=== FILE: pinchess/src/Pinchess.App/SchemeForm.cs ===








using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Scheme;
using Pinchess.Engine.Sim;

namespace Pinchess.App;

/// <summary>
/// S10: окно схемы по стандарту 2.4 — глифы, ортогональные трассы, палитра (тёплая/холодная),
/// маркеры проблем, слои (новые/снятые/байпасы/деления), «было/стало» diff, зум/пан, экспорт PNG ×2.
/// </summary>
public sealed class SchemeForm : Form
{
    private SchemeLayout _layout;
    private SchemeOptions _options = new();
    private readonly ProjectSpec _project;
    private readonly State _state;
    private readonly SimResult? _sim;
    private readonly State? _base;
    private double _zoom = 1.0;
    private System.Drawing.Point _panStart;
    private System.Drawing.Point _panOffset = System.Drawing.Point.Empty;
    private bool _panning;
    private readonly CheckBox _chkNew, _chkRemoved, _chkBypass, _chkDivider, _chkLabels, _chkFlow;
    private System.Windows.Forms.Timer? _flowTimer;
    private double _flowPhase;
    private sealed class CanvasPanel : Panel
    {
        public CanvasPanel() { DoubleBuffered = true; BackColor = System.Drawing.Color.FromArgb(248, 248, 252); Dock = DockStyle.Fill; }
    }

    private readonly Panel _canvas = new CanvasPanel();

    public SchemeForm(ProjectSpec project, State state, SimResult? sim, State? baseState, string title)
    {
        Text = title;
        StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterParent;
        Size = new Size(1280, 780);
        _project = project; _state = state; _sim = sim; _base = baseState;
        _layout = SchemeLayoutBuilder.Build(project, state, sim, baseState, _options);

        var top = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Top, Height = 64, Padding = new Padding(6) };
        var btnFit = new Button { Text = "Вписать", Width = 80 };
        btnFit.Click += (s, e) => Fit();
        var btn100 = new Button { Text = "100 %", Width = 60 };
        btn100.Click += (s, e) => { _zoom = 1.0; _panOffset = Point.Empty; _canvas.Invalidate(); };
        var btnPng = new Button { Text = "PNG ×2", Width = 90 };
        btnPng.Click += (s, e) => ExportPng();
        var btnSvg = new Button { Text = "SVG", Width = 60, Left = 0, Top = 0 };
        btnSvg.Click += (s, e) => ExportSvg();
        _chkNew = new CheckBox { Text = "Новые связи", Checked = true, AutoSize = true };
        _chkRemoved = new CheckBox { Text = "Снятые", Checked = true, AutoSize = true };
        _chkBypass = new CheckBox { Text = "Байпасы", Checked = true, AutoSize = true };
        _chkDivider = new CheckBox { Text = "Деления", Checked = true, AutoSize = true };
        _chkLabels = new CheckBox { Text = "Подписи", Checked = true, AutoSize = true };
        _chkFlow = new CheckBox { Text = "Анимация потока", Checked = true, AutoSize = true };
        _chkNew.CheckedChanged += (_, _) => _canvas.Invalidate();
        _chkRemoved.CheckedChanged += (_, _) => _canvas.Invalidate();
        _chkBypass.CheckedChanged += (_, _) => _canvas.Invalidate();
        _chkDivider.CheckedChanged += (_, _) => _canvas.Invalidate();
        _chkLabels.CheckedChanged += (_, _) => _canvas.Invalidate();
        top.Controls.Add(btnFit); top.Controls.Add(btn100); top.Controls.Add(btnPng); top.Controls.Add(btnSvg); // S1 (ревью-3, 1.7)
        top.Controls.Add(_chkNew); top.Controls.Add(_chkRemoved); top.Controls.Add(_chkBypass);
        top.Controls.Add(_chkDivider); top.Controls.Add(_chkLabels); top.Controls.Add(_chkFlow);
        btnFit.Location = new Point(6, 10); btn100.Location = new Point(92, 10); btnPng.Location = new Point(158, 10);
        btnSvg.Location = new Point(254, 10);
        _chkNew.Location = new Point(330, 12); _chkRemoved.Location = new Point(450, 12); _chkBypass.Location = new Point(540, 12);
        _chkDivider.Location = new Point(630, 12); _chkLabels.Location = new Point(720, 12);

        _flowTimer = new System.Windows.Forms.Timer { Interval = 120 };
        _flowTimer.Tick += (s, e) =>
        {
            if (!_chkFlow.Checked) return;
            _flowPhase = (_flowPhase + 0.08) % 1.0;
            _canvas.Invalidate();
        };
        _flowTimer.Start();
        _canvas.Paint += (s, e) => Paint(e.Graphics);
        _canvas.MouseWheel += (s, e) =>
        {
            if ((ModifierKeys & Keys.Control) == Keys.Control)
            {
                double factor = e.Delta > 0 ? 1.1 : 1 / 1.1;
                _zoom = Math.Clamp(_zoom * factor, 0.2, 4.0);
                _canvas.Invalidate();
            }
        };
        _canvas.MouseDown += (s, e) => { if (e.Button == MouseButtons.Left) { _panning = true; _panStart = e.Location; } };
        _canvas.MouseMove += (s, e) =>
        {
            if (!_panning) return;
            _panOffset.X += e.X - _panStart.X;
            _panOffset.Y += e.Y - _panStart.Y;
            _panStart = e.Location;
            _canvas.Invalidate();
        };
        _canvas.MouseUp += (s, e) => _panning = false;

        Controls.Add(_canvas);
        Controls.Add(top);

        var info = new Label
        {
            Dock = DockStyle.Bottom, Height = 24,
            Text = $"М1 = {_layout.MountChanges} · новых {_layout.NewLinks} · снятых {_layout.RemovedLinks} · изменённых {_layout.ChangedLinks} · зум: Ctrl+колесо, пан: ЛКМ"
        };
        Controls.Add(info);
        info.BringToFront();
    }

    /// <summary>S11: подсветка аппарата (связь статистика ↔ схема) — пересборка layout с опцией.</summary>
    public void SetHighlight(string? appId)
    {
        _options = _options with { HighlightApparatusId = appId };
        _layout = SchemeLayoutBuilder.Build(_project, _state, _sim, _base, _options);
        _canvas.Invalidate();
    }

    private void Fit()
    {
        double zx = _canvas.Width / _layout.WidthPx;
        double zy = _canvas.Height / _layout.HeightPx;
        _zoom = Math.Min(zx, zy);
        _panOffset = Point.Empty;
        _canvas.Invalidate();
    }

    private void ExportSvg()
    {
        // S14: экспорт схемы в векторный SVG (стандарт 2.4; QGIS/Inkscape/браузер)
        using var dlg = new SaveFileDialog { Filter = "SVG|*.svg", FileName = "scheme.svg" };
        if (dlg.ShowDialog(this) != DialogResult.OK) return;
        System.IO.File.WriteAllText(dlg.FileName, Pinchess.Engine.Scheme.SchemeSvg.ToSvg(_layout));
        MessageBox.Show(this, $"SVG сохранён: {dlg.FileName}", "Экспорт SVG (S14)", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
    }

    private void ExportPng()
    {
        using var dlg = new SaveFileDialog { Filter = "PNG|*.png", FileName = "scheme.png" };
        if (dlg.ShowDialog(this) != DialogResult.OK) return;
        int scale = 2;
        using var bmp = new Bitmap((int)(_layout.WidthPx * _zoom * scale), (int)(_layout.HeightPx * _zoom * scale));
        using var g = Graphics.FromImage(bmp);
        g.Clear(Color.White);
        SchemePainter.Draw(g, _layout, _zoom * scale, Point.Empty, visible: VisibleStyles(), showLabels: _chkLabels.Checked);
        bmp.Save(dlg.FileName, System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat.Png);
        System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"Схема экспортирована: {dlg.FileName}");
    }

    private static readonly RouteStyle[] AllStyles = Enum.GetValues<RouteStyle>().ToArray();

    private HashSet<RouteStyle> VisibleStyles()
    {
        var set = new HashSet<RouteStyle>(AllStyles);
        if (!_chkNew.Checked) set.Remove(RouteStyle.NewLink);
        if (!_chkRemoved.Checked) set.Remove(RouteStyle.Removed);
        if (!_chkBypass.Checked) set.Remove(RouteStyle.Bypass);
        if (!_chkDivider.Checked) set.Remove(RouteStyle.Divider);
        return set;
    }

    private void Paint(Graphics g)
    {
        g.Clear(Color.FromArgb(248, 248, 252));
        g.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
        SchemePainter.Draw(g, _layout, _zoom, _panOffset, VisibleStyles(), _chkLabels.Checked, _flowPhase, _chkFlow.Checked);
    }
}

/// <summary>Отрисовка layout на Graphics (только компиляция вне Windows; исполнение — у владельца).</summary>
public static class SchemePainter
{
    private static readonly Color HotColor = Color.FromArgb(200, 60, 20);
    private static readonly Color ColdColor = Color.FromArgb(20, 70, 190);
    private static readonly Color NewColor = Color.FromArgb(0, 140, 60);
    private static readonly Color ChangedColor = Color.FromArgb(200, 120, 0);
    private static readonly Color RemovedColor = Color.FromArgb(120, 120, 120);
    private static readonly Color BypassColor = Color.FromArgb(130, 130, 160);
    private static readonly Color DividerColor = Color.FromArgb(140, 60, 160);
    private static readonly Color GlyphFill = Color.FromArgb(255, 255, 250);
    private static readonly Color GlyphBorder = Color.FromArgb(60, 60, 70);
    private static readonly Color PinchColor = Color.FromArgb(230, 140, 0);
    private static readonly Color SleepingColor = Color.FromArgb(160, 160, 160);

    public static void Draw(Graphics g, SchemeLayout l, double zoom, Point pan, HashSet<RouteStyle> visible, bool showLabels, double flowPhase = 0.0, bool animateFlow = false)
    {
        using var font = new Font("Segoe UI", 8f);
        using var fontBold = new Font("Segoe UI", 9f, FontStyle.Bold);

        // трассы
        foreach (var seg in l.Segments)
        {
            if (!visible.Contains(seg.Style)) continue;
            var color = seg.IsHot ? HotColor : ColdColor;
            float width = 2.2f;
            Pen pen;
            switch (seg.Style)
            {
                case RouteStyle.NewLink: color = NewColor; width = 3.2f; pen = PenFor(color, width, dash: false); break;
                case RouteStyle.Changed: color = ChangedColor; width = 2.8f; pen = PenFor(color, width, dash: false); break;
                case RouteStyle.Bypass: color = BypassColor; width = 1.6f; pen = PenFor(color, width, dash: true); break;
                case RouteStyle.Removed: color = RemovedColor; width = 1.6f; pen = PenFor(color, width, dash: true); break;
                case RouteStyle.Divider: color = DividerColor; width = 2.2f; pen = PenFor(color, width, dash: false); break;
                case RouteStyle.Branch: color = color; width = 2.2f; pen = PenFor(color, width, dash: false); break;
                default: pen = PenFor(color, width, dash: false); break;
            }
            float x1 = (float)(seg.X1 * zoom + pan.X), y1 = (float)(seg.Y1 * zoom + pan.Y);
            float x2 = (float)(seg.X2 * zoom + pan.X), y2 = (float)(seg.Y2 * zoom + pan.Y);
            g.DrawLine(pen, x1, y1, x2, y2);
            // стрелки направления
            bool horizontal = Math.Abs(y1 - y2) < 0.5f;
            float ax = horizontal ? (x2 > x1 ? x2 - 5 : x2 + 5) : x2;
            float ay = horizontal ? y2 : (y2 > y1 ? y2 - 5 : y2 + 5);
            if (horizontal)
                g.DrawLines(pen, new[] { new PointF(ax - 4, ay - 3), new PointF(ax, ay), new PointF(ax - 4, ay + 3) });
            else
                g.DrawLines(pen, new[] { new PointF(ax - 3, ay - 4), new PointF(ax, ay), new PointF(ax + 3, ay - 4) });
            if (seg.Label is not null && showLabels)
                g.DrawString(seg.Label, font, Brushes.Black, (x1 + x2) / 2 + 4, (y1 + y2) / 2 - 8);
            // UI2b (v20): анимация потока — бегущие маркеры вдоль активных связей (новые/изменённые)
            if (animateFlow && (seg.Style == RouteStyle.NewLink || seg.Style == RouteStyle.Changed))
            {
                float dx = x2 - x1, dy = y2 - y1;
                double len = Math.Sqrt((double)dx * dx + (double)dy * dy);
                if (len > 6)
                    for (int k = 0; k < 3; k++)
                    {
                        double ppos = (flowPhase + k / 3.0) % 1.0;
                        float mx = x1 + (float)(dx * ppos), my = y1 + (float)(dy * ppos);
                        using var brush = new SolidBrush(Color.FromArgb(160, color));
                        g.FillEllipse(brush, mx - 2.5f, my - 2.5f, 5f, 5f);
                    }
            }
        }

        // глифы
        foreach (var n in l.Nodes)
        {
            float x = (float)(n.X * zoom + pan.X), y = (float)(n.Y * zoom + pan.Y);
            float w = (float)(n.W * zoom), h = (float)(n.H * zoom);
            var border = n.Flag switch
            {
                NodeFlag.Pinch => PinchColor,
                NodeFlag.Sleeping => SleepingColor,
                _ => GlyphBorder,
            };
            using (var fill = new SolidBrush(GlyphFill))
                g.FillRectangle(fill, x, y, w, h);
            using (var pb = new Pen(border, 1.8f))
                g.DrawRectangle(pb, x, y, w, h);
            // цветная рамка для новых аппаратов (стандарт 2.4.2)
            if (!n.IsExisting)
                using (var pb = new Pen(NewColor, 2.6f))
                    g.DrawRectangle(pb, x - 2, y - 2, w + 4, h + 4);
            // обязательный — маркер «!»
            if (n.IsMandatory)
                g.DrawString("!", fontBold, Brushes.DarkRed, x + w - 10, y + 2);
            // трубная/межтрубная разметка: горизонтальная полоса — горячая сторона, вертикальная — холодная
            using (var hb = new Pen(HotColor, 2.4f))
                g.DrawLine(hb, x + 4, y + h * 0.35f, x + w - 4, y + h * 0.35f);
            using (var cb = new Pen(ColdColor, 2.4f))
                g.DrawLine(cb, x + w / 2, y + h * 0.55f, x + w / 2, y + h - 4);
            // подписи: аппаратный Id + K/площадь; температуры вход/выход
            g.DrawString(n.ApparatusId, fontBold, Brushes.Black, x + 4, y + 2);
            if (n.HotFlowId is not null && n.ThInC is not null && n.ThOutC is not null && showLabels)
                g.DrawString($"{n.ThInC:0.0}→{n.ThOutC:0.0}", font, Brushes.Black, x + 4, y + h * 0.5f);
            if (n.ColdFlowId is not null && n.TcInC is not null && n.TcOutC is not null && showLabels)
                g.DrawString($"{n.TcInC:0.0}→{n.TcOutC:0.0}", font, Brushes.Black, x + 4, y + h - 14);
            if (n.Flag == NodeFlag.Pinch && showLabels)
                g.DrawString("ΔT<Tmin", font, new SolidBrush(PinchColor), x + w + 2, y + 2);
        }

        // подписи
        if (showLabels)
        {
            foreach (var lb in l.Labels)
            {
                float x = (float)(lb.X * zoom + pan.X), y = (float)(lb.Y * zoom + pan.Y);
                g.DrawString(lb.Text, lb.Bold ? fontBold : font, lb.IsHot ? Brushes.Maroon : Brushes.Navy, x, y);
            }
        }

        // S11: подсветка выбранного аппарата (связь статистика ↔ схема)
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(l.HighlightId))
            foreach (var n in l.Nodes.Where(x => x.ApparatusId == l.HighlightId))
            {
                float x = (float)(n.X * zoom + pan.X), y = (float)(n.Y * zoom + pan.Y);
                float w = (float)(n.W * zoom), h = (float)(n.H * zoom);
                using (var hp = new Pen(Color.Gold, 3.2f))
                    g.DrawRectangle(hp, x - 4, y - 4, w + 8, h + 8);
            }

        // заголовок и М1
        g.DrawString(l.Title, fontBold, Brushes.Black, 8, (float)(l.HeightPx * zoom - 22));
    }

    private static Pen PenFor(Color c, float w, bool dash)
    {
        var p = new Pen(c, w);
        if (dash) p.DashStyle = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.DashStyle.Dash;
        return p;
    }
}








=== FILE: pinchess/src/Pinchess.App/SchemeGridForm.cs ===








using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Sim;
using Pinchess.Engine.Grid;

namespace Pinchess.App;

/// <summary>
/// Сетчатая диаграмма — ввод (UI2a): потоки — линии-полоски (горячие/холодные), аппараты —
/// кружки на обеих кривых (связаны пунктиром), у кружков после «Рассчитать» — крайние
/// температуры до/после, на линиях — расход у начала. Результат ввода — в таблицу
/// (GridTableForm). Разветвления — следующий шаг UI2b. Смоук — у владельца.
/// </summary>
public sealed class SchemeGridForm : Form
{
    private readonly ProjectSpec _project;
    private readonly List<GridLine> _lines = new();
    private readonly List<GridApp> _apps = new();
    private readonly Panel _canvas = new();
    private readonly Label _modeLabel = new();
    private int _tool; // 0=поток 1=аппарат 2=правка 3=удалить
    private GridLine? _draft;
    private (GridLine Line, int Seg, double T, GridPoint P)? _pendingFirst;
    private int? _dragLine; private int? _dragSeg; private int? _dragApp; private string? _dragSide;

    /// <summary>S1 (ревью-3, 1.10): true после «Применить к проекту» — основная форма сбрасывает последний скан.</summary>
    public bool ConfigurationApplied { get; private set; }

    public SchemeGridForm(ProjectSpec project)
    {
        _project = project;
        Text = "Сетчатая диаграмма — ввод (UI2a)";
        StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterParent;
        Size = new Size(1180, 720);
        MinimumSize = new Size(900, 560);

        var tools = new FlowLayoutPanel { Dock = DockStyle.Top, Height = 66, Padding = new Padding(8, 6, 8, 0) };
        AddTool(tools, "Поток", 0);
        AddTool(tools, "Аппарат", 1);
        AddTool(tools, "Правка", 2);
        AddTool(tools, "Удалить", 3);
        var calcBtn = new Button { Text = "Рассчитать", Width = 110 };
        calcBtn.Click += (s, e) => CalculateTemps();
        var tableBtn = new Button { Text = "В таблицу", Width = 110 };
        tableBtn.Click += (s, e) => OpenTable();
        var saveBtn = new Button { Text = "Сохранить…", Width = 110 };
        saveBtn.Click += (s, e) => SaveDiagram();
        var openBtn = new Button { Text = "Открыть…", Width = 100 };
        openBtn.Click += (s, e) => LoadDiagram();
        _modeLabel.Text = "Инструмент: Поток — клики рисуют линию, двойной клик завершает.";
        _modeLabel.AutoSize = true;
        _modeLabel.ForeColor = Color.DimGray;
        _modeLabel.Margin = new Padding(6, 10, 0, 0);
        tools.Controls.AddRange(new Control[] { calcBtn, tableBtn, saveBtn, openBtn, _modeLabel });

        _canvas.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
        _canvas.AutoScroll = true;
        _canvas.BackColor = Color.White;
        _canvas.Paint += CanvasPaint;
        _canvas.MouseClick += CanvasClick;
        _canvas.MouseDoubleClick += CanvasDoubleClick;
        _canvas.MouseDown += CanvasDown;
        _canvas.MouseMove += CanvasMove;
        _canvas.MouseUp += CanvasUp;

        Controls.Add(_canvas);
        Controls.Add(tools);
    }

    private void AddTool(FlowLayoutPanel p, string title, int tool)
    {
        var b = new Button { Text = title, Width = 100 };
        b.Click += (s, e) =>
        {
            _tool = tool;
            FinishDraft();
            _pendingFirst = null;
            _modeLabel.Text = "Инструмент: " + title + " — " + (tool switch
            {
                0 => "клики рисуют линию потока, двойной клик завершает.",
                1 => "клик по горячей линии, затем по холодной — создаётся аппарат.",
                2 => "клик по линии/кружку — свойства; перетаскивание вершин.",
                _ => "клик по кружку/линии — удалить.",
            });
            _canvas.Invalidate();
        };
        p.Controls.Add(b);
    }

    // ---------- UI2b (v19): snap-to-grid + undo/redo ----------
    private const double GridStep = 10.0;

    private static GridPoint SnapPoint(GridPoint p) =>
        new(Math.Round(p.X / GridStep) * GridStep, Math.Round(p.Y / GridStep) * GridStep);

    private static double SnapT(double t) => Math.Clamp(Math.Round(t / 0.05) * 0.05, 0.0, 1.0);

    private readonly Stack<(List<GridLine> Lines, List<GridApp> Apps)> _undo = new();
    private readonly Stack<(List<GridLine> Lines, List<GridApp> Apps)> _redo = new();

    private static List<GridLine> CloneLines(IEnumerable<GridLine> src) =>
        src.Select(l => new GridLine
        {
            Id = l.Id, Name = l.Name, IsHot = l.IsHot, EnvId = l.EnvId,
            RateKgH = l.RateKgH, TStart = l.TStart, TTarget = l.TTarget,
            DpBudget = l.DpBudget, AllowSplit = l.AllowSplit, MaxSplits = l.MaxSplits,
            Points = l.Points.Select(q => new GridPoint(q.X, q.Y)).ToList(),
        }).ToList();

    private static List<GridApp> CloneApps(IEnumerable<GridApp> src) =>
        src.Select(a => new GridApp
        {
            Id = a.Id, Name = a.Name, AreaM2 = a.AreaM2, K0 = a.K0,
            TubeDiMm = a.TubeDiMm, NomVhMs = a.NomVhMs, NomVcMs = a.NomVcMs,
            IsMandatory = a.IsMandatory, IsExisting = a.IsExisting,
            HotLineId = a.HotLineId, HotSeg = a.HotSeg, HotT = a.HotT,
            ColdLineId = a.ColdLineId, ColdSeg = a.ColdSeg, ColdT = a.ColdT,
        }).ToList();

    private void PushUndo()
    {
        _undo.Push((CloneLines(_lines), CloneApps(_apps)));
        _redo.Clear();
    }

    private void Undo()
    {
        if (_undo.Count == 0) return;
        _redo.Push((CloneLines(_lines), CloneApps(_apps)));
        var (l, a) = _undo.Pop();
        _lines.Clear(); _lines.AddRange(l);
        _apps.Clear(); _apps.AddRange(a);
        _canvas.Invalidate();
        _modeLabel.Text = "Undo: отменено последнее действие.";
    }

    private void Redo()
    {
        if (_redo.Count == 0) return;
        _undo.Push((CloneLines(_lines), CloneApps(_apps)));
        var (l, a) = _redo.Pop();
        _lines.Clear(); _lines.AddRange(l);
        _apps.Clear(); _apps.AddRange(a);
        _canvas.Invalidate();
        _modeLabel.Text = "Redo: действие возвращено.";
    }

    protected override bool ProcessCmdKey(ref Message msg, Keys keyData)
    {
        if (keyData == (Keys.Control | Keys.Z)) { Undo(); return true; }
        if (keyData == (Keys.Control | Keys.Y)) { Redo(); return true; }
        return base.ProcessCmdKey(ref msg, keyData);
    }

    // ---------- геометрия ----------
    private static (double Dist, double T) ProjectOnSeg(GridPoint p, GridPoint a, GridPoint b)
    {
        double dx = b.X - a.X, dy = b.Y - a.Y;
        double len2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;
        if (len2 < 1e-9) return (Math.Sqrt((p.X - a.X) * (p.X - a.X) + (p.Y - a.Y) * (p.Y - a.Y)), 0);
        double t = ((p.X - a.X) * dx + (p.Y - a.Y) * dy) / len2;
        t = Math.Max(0, Math.Min(1, t));
        double px = a.X + t * dx, py = a.Y + t * dy;
        return (Math.Sqrt((p.X - px) * (p.X - px) + (p.Y - py) * (p.Y - py)), t);
    }

    private (GridLine? Line, int Seg, double T, GridPoint P) HitLine(GridPoint p)
    {
        GridLine? best = null; int bestSeg = 0; double bestT = 0; double bestDist = 10; GridPoint bestP = new(0, 0);
        foreach (var line in _lines)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i + 1 < line.Points.Count; i++)
            {
                var (dist, t) = ProjectOnSeg(p, line.Points[i], line.Points[i + 1]);
                if (dist < bestDist)
                {
                    bestDist = dist; best = line; bestSeg = i; bestT = t;
                    bestP = new GridPoint(line.Points[i].X + t * (line.Points[i + 1].X - line.Points[i].X),
                                          line.Points[i].Y + t * (line.Points[i + 1].Y - line.Points[i].Y));
                }
            }
        }
        return (best, bestSeg, bestT, bestP);
    }

    private (GridApp? App, string Side) HitApp(GridPoint p)
    {
        foreach (var a in _apps)
        {
            var (hl, hs, _) = Resolve(a.HotLineId, a.HotSeg, a.HotT);
            var (cl, cs, _) = Resolve(a.ColdLineId, a.ColdSeg, a.ColdT);
            if (hl is not null)
            {
                var pt = PointOn(hl.Points, hs, a.HotT);
                if (Math.Abs(pt.X - p.X) < 11 && Math.Abs(pt.Y - p.Y) < 11) return (a, "hot");
            }
            if (cl is not null)
            {
                var pt = PointOn(cl.Points, cs, a.ColdT);
                if (Math.Abs(pt.X - p.X) < 11 && Math.Abs(pt.Y - p.Y) < 11) return (a, "cold");
            }
        }
        return (null, "");
    }

    private (GridLine?, int, double) Resolve(string lineId, int seg, double t)
    {
        var line = _lines.FirstOrDefault(l => l.Id == lineId);
        if (line is null || seg < 0 || seg + 1 >= line.Points.Count) return (null, seg, t);
        return (line, seg, t);
    }

    private static GridPoint PointOn(List<GridPoint> pts, int seg, double t)
    {
        var a = pts[seg]; var b = pts[seg + 1];
        return new GridPoint(a.X + t * (b.X - a.X), a.Y + t * (b.Y - a.Y));
    }

    // ---------- инструменты ----------
    private void CanvasClick(object? sender, MouseEventArgs e)
    {
        if (_draft is not null) return;
        var p = new GridPoint(e.X - _canvas.AutoScrollPosition.X, e.Y - _canvas.AutoScrollPosition.Y); // S1 (ревью-3, 1.9): world = client − scroll;
        switch (_tool)
        {
            case 1:
                HandleApparatusClick(p);
                break;
            case 2:
                EditAt(p);
                break;
            case 3:
                DeleteAt(p);
                break;
        }
        _canvas.Invalidate();
    }

    private void HandleApparatusClick(GridPoint p)
    {
        var (line, seg, t, _) = HitLine(p);
        if (line is null) return;
        if (_pendingFirst is null)
        {
            _pendingFirst = (line, seg, t, p);
            return;
        }
        if (line.IsHot == _pendingFirst.Value.Line.IsHot)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(this, "Выберите линию противоположной стороны (горячая ↔ холодная).",
                "Аппарат", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
            _pendingFirst = null;
            return;
        }
        (GridLine l1, int s1, double t1) = _pendingFirst.Value.Line.IsHot
            ? (_pendingFirst.Value.Line, _pendingFirst.Value.Seg, _pendingFirst.Value.T)
            : (line, seg, t);
        (GridLine l2, int s2, double t2) = _pendingFirst.Value.Line.IsHot
            ? (line, seg, t)
            : (_pendingFirst.Value.Line, _pendingFirst.Value.Seg, _pendingFirst.Value.T);
        _pendingFirst = null;
        using var dlg = new AppPropsDialog(_apps.Count + 1);
        if (dlg.ShowDialog(this) != DialogResult.OK) return;
        string id = dlg.UniqueId(_apps.Select(x => x.Id).ToHashSet());
        PushUndo(); // v19: добавление аппарата — шаг undo
        _apps.Add(new GridApp
        {
            Id = id,
            Name = dlg.AppName,
            AreaM2 = dlg.Area,
            K0 = dlg.K0,
            TubeDiMm = dlg.TubeDi,
            NomVhMs = dlg.VHot,
            NomVcMs = dlg.VCold,
            IsMandatory = dlg.IsMandatory,
            IsExisting = dlg.IsExisting,
            HotLineId = l1.Id, HotSeg = s1, HotT = t1,
            ColdLineId = l2.Id, ColdSeg = s2, ColdT = t2,
        });
    }

    private void EditAt(GridPoint p)
    {
        var (app, _) = HitApp(p);
        if (app is not null)
        {
            using var dlg = new AppPropsDialog(app.Id, app);
            if (dlg.ShowDialog(this) == DialogResult.OK)
            {
                PushUndo(); // v19: правка аппарата — шаг undo
                app.Name = dlg.AppName;
                app.AreaM2 = dlg.Area;
                app.K0 = dlg.K0;
                app.TubeDiMm = dlg.TubeDi;
                app.NomVhMs = dlg.VHot;
                app.NomVcMs = dlg.VCold;
                app.IsMandatory = dlg.IsMandatory;
                app.IsExisting = dlg.IsExisting;
                app.HasTemps = false;
            }
            return;
        }
        var (line, _, _, _) = HitLine(p);
        if (line is not null)
        {
            using var dlg = new FlowPropsDialog(_project, line);
            if (dlg.ShowDialog(this) == DialogResult.OK)
                dlg.WriteTo(line);
        }
    }

    private void DeleteAt(GridPoint p)
    {
        var (app, _) = HitApp(p);
        if (app is not null) { PushUndo(); _apps.Remove(app); return; } // v19: удаление — шаг undo
        var (line, _, _, _) = HitLine(p);
        if (line is not null)
        {
            PushUndo(); // v19: удаление линии (с аппаратами) — шаг undo
            _lines.Remove(line);
            _apps.RemoveAll(a => a.HotLineId == line.Id || a.ColdLineId == line.Id);
        }
    }

    private void CanvasDown(object? sender, MouseEventArgs e)
    {
        var p = new GridPoint(e.X - _canvas.AutoScrollPosition.X, e.Y - _canvas.AutoScrollPosition.Y); // S1 (ревью-3, 1.9): world = client − scroll;
        if (_tool == 0)
        {
            if (_draft is null)
                _draft = new GridLine { Points = { p } };
            else
            {
                var last = _draft.Points[^1];
                if (Math.Abs(p.X - last.X) > 3 || Math.Abs(p.Y - last.Y) > 3) _draft.Points.Add(p);
            }
            _canvas.Invalidate();
            return;
        }
        if (_tool == 2 && e.Button == MouseButtons.Left)
        {
            var (app, side) = HitApp(p);
            if (app is not null)
            {
                _dragApp = _apps.IndexOf(app);
                _dragSide = side;
                // S1 (ревью-3, 1.8): снапшот ДО первой мутации (CanvasMove двигает сразу)
                PushUndo();
                return;
            }
            var (line, seg, _, _) = HitLine(p);
            if (line is not null)
            {
                _dragLine = _lines.IndexOf(line);
                _dragSeg = seg;
                // S1 (ревью-3, 1.8): снапшот ДО первой мутации
                PushUndo();
            }
        }
    }

    private void CanvasMove(object? sender, MouseEventArgs e)
    {
        if (_tool != 2) return;
        var p = new GridPoint(e.X - _canvas.AutoScrollPosition.X, e.Y - _canvas.AutoScrollPosition.Y); // S1 (ревью-3, 1.9): world = client − scroll;
        if (_dragApp is int ai && _dragSide is string ds && ai >= 0 && ai < _apps.Count)
        {
            var a = _apps[ai];
            var hit = HitLine(p);
            if (hit.Line is { } hl && ds == "hot" && hl.Id == a.HotLineId) { a.HotSeg = hit.Seg; a.HotT = SnapT(hit.T); }
            else if (hit.Line is { } hl2 && ds == "cold" && hl2.Id == a.ColdLineId) { a.ColdSeg = hit.Seg; a.ColdT = SnapT(hit.T); }
            _canvas.Invalidate();
            return;
        }
        if (_dragLine is int li && _dragSeg is int seg && li >= 0 && li < _lines.Count)
        {
            var line = _lines[li];
            if (seg < line.Points.Count) { line.Points[seg] = SnapPoint(p); _canvas.Invalidate(); }
        }
    }

    private void CanvasUp(object? sender, MouseEventArgs e)
    {
        // S1 (ревью-3, 1.8): снапшот сделан в CanvasDown ДО мутации; если перемещение
        // ничего не изменило — шаг снимается (Undo не должен возвращать то же состояние)
        if (_dragApp is not null || _dragLine is not null)
        {
            if (_undo.Count > 0 && _undo.TryPeek(out var before) &&
                SameSnapshot(before.Lines, before.Apps, _lines, _apps))
                _undo.Pop();
        }
        _dragApp = null; _dragLine = null; _dragSeg = null; _dragSide = null;
    }

    /// <summary>S1 (ревью-3, 1.8): сравнение снапшота с текущим состоянием (координаты + привязки).</summary>
    private static bool SameSnapshot(IReadOnlyList<GridLine> aLines, IReadOnlyList<GridApp> aApps,
        IReadOnlyList<GridLine> bLines, IReadOnlyList<GridApp> bApps)
    {
        if (aLines.Count != bLines.Count || aApps.Count != bApps.Count) return false;
        for (int i = 0; i < aLines.Count; i++)
        {
            var la = aLines[i]; var lb = bLines[i];
            if (la.Id != lb.Id || la.Points.Count != lb.Points.Count) return false;
            for (int j = 0; j < la.Points.Count; j++)
                if (Math.Abs(la.Points[j].X - lb.Points[j].X) > 1e-9 || Math.Abs(la.Points[j].Y - lb.Points[j].Y) > 1e-9) return false;
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < aApps.Count; i++)
        {
            var aa = aApps[i]; var ab = bApps[i];
            if (aa.Id != ab.Id || aa.HotSeg != ab.HotSeg || aa.ColdSeg != ab.ColdSeg) return false;
            if (Math.Abs(aa.HotT - ab.HotT) > 1e-9 || Math.Abs(aa.ColdT - ab.ColdT) > 1e-9) return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    private void CanvasDoubleClick(object? sender, MouseEventArgs e)
    {
        if (_tool != 0 || _draft is null) return;
        FinishDraft();
    }

    private void FinishDraft()
    {
        if (_draft is null) return;
        var draft = _draft;
        _draft = null;
        if (draft.Points.Count < 2) return;
        using var dlg = new FlowPropsDialog(_project, (GridLine?)null);
        if (dlg.ShowDialog(this) != DialogResult.OK) return;
        // S1 (ревью-3, 1.8): снапшот ДО мутации — Undo реально откатывает добавление линии
        PushUndo();
        _lines.Add(new GridLine
        {
            Id = dlg.UniqueId(_lines.Select(l => l.Id).ToHashSet()),
            Name = dlg.FlowName,
            IsHot = dlg.IsHot,
            EnvId = dlg.EnvId,
            RateKgH = dlg.Rate,
            TStart = dlg.TStart,
            TTarget = dlg.TTarget,
            DpBudget = dlg.DpBudget,
            AllowSplit = dlg.AllowSplit,
            MaxSplits = dlg.MaxSplits,
            Points = draft.Points.Select(SnapPoint).ToList(), // UI2b: snap
        });
        _canvas.Invalidate();
    }

    // ---------- построение проекта из диаграммы ----------
    internal ProjectSpec BuildProject() => GridProjectMapper.BuildProject(_project, _lines, _apps);

    internal List<AppInstance> BuildInstances(ProjectSpec spec) => GridProjectMapper.BuildInstances(spec, _lines, _apps);

    private void CalculateTemps()
    {
        if (_lines.Count == 0 || _apps.Count == 0)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(this, "Нужны хотя бы одна линия и один аппарат.", "Расчёт", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
            return;
        }
        foreach (var a in _apps) a.HasTemps = false;
        var spec = BuildProject();
        var report = Engine.Validation.ProjectValidator.Validate(spec);
        if (report.HasErrors)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(this, report.ToString(), "Расчёт — ошибки проекта", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
            return;
        }
        var instances = BuildInstances(spec);
        var sim = new Simulator(spec);
        var res = sim.Simulate(instances, SimLevel.Coarse);
        foreach (var r in res.Apparatus)
        {
            var a = _apps.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Id == r.ApparatusId);
            if (a is null) continue;
            a.ThIn = r.ThInC; a.ThOut = r.ThOutC;
            a.TcIn = r.TcInC; a.TcOut = r.TcOutC;
            a.HasTemps = true;
        }
        _canvas.Invalidate();
        MessageBox.Show(this,
            $"Расчёт (coarse): Q = {res.QTotalMw:0.000} МВт, сходимость {(res.AllConverged ? "да" : "нет")}.\nКрайние температуры подписаны у кружков.",
            "Сетчатая диаграмма — расчёт", MessageBoxButtons.OK,
            res.AllConverged ? MessageBoxIcon.Information : MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
        Engine.Log.LogMiss.Evt("SIM", $"Сетчатая диаграмма: Q={res.QTotalMw:0.000} МВт");
    }

    private void OpenTable()
    {
        var spec = BuildProject();
        using var table = new GridTableForm(_project, spec, BuildInstances(spec), _lines, _apps);
        if (table.ShowDialog(this) == DialogResult.OK)
        {
            // S1 (ревью-3, 1.10): Apply изменил проект — основной форме нужен сигнал
            // об инвалидации последнего скана (ReferenceEquals больше не сравнивает актуальность)
            ConfigurationApplied = true;
            _canvas.Invalidate();
        }
    }

    private void SaveDiagram()
    {
        using var dlg = new SaveFileDialog { Filter = "Диаграмма Pinchess (*.grid.json)|*.grid.json", FileName = "схема.grid.json" };
        if (dlg.ShowDialog(this) != DialogResult.OK) return;
        var dto = new DiagramDto(_lines, _apps);
        System.IO.File.WriteAllText(dlg.FileName, System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer.Serialize(dto),
            new System.Text.UTF8Encoding(false));
        MessageBox.Show(this, "Диаграмма сохранена: " + dlg.FileName, "Сохранение", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
    }

    private void LoadDiagram()
    {
        using var dlg = new OpenFileDialog { Filter = "Диаграмма Pinchess (*.grid.json)|*.grid.json|Все файлы (*.*)|*.*" };
        if (dlg.ShowDialog(this) != DialogResult.OK) return;
        try
        {
            var dto = System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer.Deserialize<DiagramDto>(System.IO.File.ReadAllText(dlg.FileName));
            if (dto is null) return;
            _lines.Clear();
            _lines.AddRange(dto.Lines ?? new List<GridLine>());
            _apps.Clear();
            _apps.AddRange(dto.Apps ?? new List<GridApp>());
            _canvas.Invalidate();
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(this, "Не удалось загрузить диаграмму: " + ex.Message, "Открытие", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
        }
    }

    private sealed record DiagramDto(List<GridLine> Lines, List<GridApp> Apps);

    // ---------- отрисовка ----------
    private void CanvasPaint(object? sender, PaintEventArgs e)
    {
        var g = e.Graphics;
        g.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
        // S1 (ревью-3, 1.9): рисование в «мировых» координатах — сдвиг на текущий скролл;
        // ввод (CanvasClick/Down/Move) согласован: world = client − AutoScrollPosition
        g.TranslateTransform(_canvas.AutoScrollPosition.X, _canvas.AutoScrollPosition.Y);
        foreach (var line in _lines)
            DrawLine(g, line);
        foreach (var a in _apps)
            DrawApp(g, a);
        if (_pendingFirst is { } pf)
            DrawMarker(g, pf.P, Color.DarkOrange);
        if (_draft is { } d && d.Points.Count > 0)
        {
            using var dash = new Pen(Color.Gray, 1.6f) { DashStyle = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.DashStyle.Dash };
            var pts = d.Points.Select(p => new Point((int)p.X, (int)p.Y)).ToArray();
            if (pts.Length > 1) g.DrawLines(dash, pts);
            var last = pts[^1];
            g.FillEllipse(Brushes.Gray, last.X - 3, last.Y - 3, 6, 6);
        }
    }

    private void DrawLine(Graphics g, GridLine line)
    {
        if (line.Points.Count < 2) return;
        using var pen = new Pen(line.IsHot ? Color.Firebrick : Color.RoyalBlue, line.IsHot ? 4f : 3.5f);
        var pts = line.Points.Select(p => new Point((int)p.X, (int)p.Y)).ToArray();
        g.DrawLines(pen, pts);
        var first = line.Points[0];
        var last = line.Points[^1];
        string head = $"{line.Id}: G = {line.RateKgH:0.#} кг/ч · {line.TStart:0.#}→{line.TTarget:0.#} °C";
        DrawLabel(g, (int)first.X, (int)first.Y - 16, head, line.IsHot ? Color.Firebrick : Color.RoyalBlue);
        DrawLabel(g, (int)last.X, (int)last.Y + 10, $"конец: {line.TTarget:0.#} °C", Color.DimGray);
    }

    private void DrawApp(Graphics g, GridApp a)
    {
        var (hl, hs, _) = Resolve(a.HotLineId, a.HotSeg, a.HotT);
        var (cl, cs, _) = Resolve(a.ColdLineId, a.ColdSeg, a.ColdT);
        if (hl is null || cl is null) return;
        var ph = PointOn(hl.Points, hs, a.HotT);
        var pc = PointOn(cl.Points, cs, a.ColdT);
        using var link = new Pen(Color.SeaGreen, 1.6f) { DashStyle = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.DashStyle.Dot };
        g.DrawLine(link, (float)ph.X, (float)ph.Y, (float)pc.X, (float)pc.Y);
        DrawCircle(g, ph, Color.Firebrick);
        DrawCircle(g, pc, Color.RoyalBlue);
        DrawLabel(g, (int)ph.X + 12, (int)ph.Y - 12, $"{a.Id} · {a.AreaM2:0.#} м² · K0 {a.K0:0.#}", Color.Firebrick);
        if (a.HasTemps)
        {
            DrawLabel(g, (int)ph.X - 12, (int)ph.Y + 12, $"{a.ThIn:0.#}→{a.ThOut:0.#} °C", Color.Firebrick);
            DrawLabel(g, (int)pc.X - 12, (int)pc.Y + 12, $"{a.TcIn:0.#}→{a.TcOut:0.#} °C", Color.RoyalBlue);
        }
    }

    private static void DrawCircle(Graphics g, GridPoint p, Color color)
    {
        using var fill = new SolidBrush(Color.FromArgb(200, color));
        g.FillEllipse(fill, (float)p.X - 7, (float)p.Y - 7, 14, 14);
        g.FillEllipse(Brushes.White, (float)p.X - 3, (float)p.Y - 3, 6, 6);
    }

    private static void DrawMarker(Graphics g, GridPoint p, Color color)
    {
        using var pen = new Pen(color, 2f);
        g.DrawRectangle(pen, (float)p.X - 10, (float)p.Y - 10, 20, 20);
    }

    internal static void DrawLabel(Graphics g, int x, int y, string text, Color color)
    {
        var size = g.MeasureString(text, Control.DefaultFont);
        using var bg = new SolidBrush(Color.FromArgb(220, Color.White));
        g.FillRectangle(bg, x, y, (int)size.Width + 4, (int)size.Height);
        using var br = new SolidBrush(color);
        g.DrawString(text, Control.DefaultFont, br, x + 2, y);
    }
}

/// <summary>Диалог свойств аппарата (создание/правка) для сетчатого ввода.</summary>
public sealed class AppPropsDialog : Form
{
    private readonly TextBox _id = new();
    private readonly TextBox _name = new();
    private readonly TextBox _area = new();
    private readonly TextBox _k0 = new();
    private readonly TextBox _tube = new();
    private readonly TextBox _vhot = new();
    private readonly TextBox _vcold = new();
    private readonly CheckBox _mandatory = new();
    private readonly CheckBox _existing = new();

    public AppPropsDialog(int index) : this($"N-{index:00}", null) { }
    public AppPropsDialog(string initialId, GridApp? app)
    {
        Text = app is null ? "Новый аппарат" : "Правка аппарата";
        StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterParent;
        Width = 390;

        var table = new TableLayoutPanel { Dock = DockStyle.Top, ColumnCount = 2, AutoSize = true, Padding = new Padding(8) };
        void AddRow(string label, Control c)
        {
            table.Controls.Add(new Label { Text = label, AutoSize = true, Margin = new Padding(3, 8, 3, 0) });
            c.Width = 220;
            table.Controls.Add(c);
        }
        AddRow("Id:", _id);
        AddRow("Наименование:", _name);
        AddRow("Площадь, м²:", _area);
        AddRow("K0, Вт/(м²·К):", _k0);
        AddRow("D трубки, мм:", _tube);
        AddRow("v гор. ном., м/с:", _vhot);
        AddRow("v хол. ном., м/с:", _vcold);
        _mandatory.Text = "Обязательный в схеме";
        _mandatory.AutoSize = true;
        table.Controls.Add(new Label()); table.Controls.Add(_mandatory);
        _existing.Text = "Существующий (иначе — новый)";
        _existing.AutoSize = true; _existing.Checked = true;
        table.Controls.Add(new Label()); table.Controls.Add(_existing);

        var ok = new Button { Text = "OK", DialogResult = DialogResult.OK, Width = 90 };
        var cancel = new Button { Text = "Отмена", DialogResult = DialogResult.Cancel, Width = 90 };
        var flow = new FlowLayoutPanel { Dock = DockStyle.Bottom, Height = 44, FlowDirection = FlowDirection.RightToLeft };
        flow.Controls.Add(ok); flow.Controls.Add(cancel);
        Controls.Add(table); Controls.Add(flow);
        AcceptButton = ok; CancelButton = cancel;

        _id.Text = initialId;
        if (app is not null)
        {
            _name.Text = app.Name;
            _area.Text = app.AreaM2.ToString("0.##");
            _k0.Text = app.K0.ToString("0.##");
            _tube.Text = app.TubeDiMm.ToString("0.##");
            _mandatory.Checked = app.IsMandatory;
            _existing.Checked = app.IsExisting;
        }
        else
        {
            _area.Text = "100"; _k0.Text = "250";
        }
        _vhot.Text = app is not null ? app.NomVhMs.ToString("0.##") : "1";
        _vcold.Text = app is not null ? app.NomVcMs.ToString("0.##") : "1";
    }

    public string AppName => _name.Text;
    public double Area => Parse(_area.Text, 100);
    public double K0 => Parse(_k0.Text, 250);
    public double TubeDi => Parse(_tube.Text, 20);
    public double VHot => Parse(_vhot.Text, 1);
    public double VCold => Parse(_vcold.Text, 1);
    public bool IsMandatory => _mandatory.Checked;
    public bool IsExisting => _existing.Checked;

    public string UniqueId(HashSet<string> taken)
    {
        string id = _id.Text.Trim();
        if (id.Length == 0 || taken.Contains(id))
        {
            int n = taken.Count + 1;
            while (taken.Contains($"N-{n:00}")) n++;
            id = $"N-{n:00}";
        }
        return id;
    }

    private static double Parse(string s, double def) =>
        double.TryParse((s ?? "").Trim().Replace(',', '.'), System.Globalization.NumberStyles.Any,
            System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double d) ? d : def;
}

/// <summary>Диалог свойств потока (создание/правка) для сетчатого ввода.</summary>
public sealed class FlowPropsDialog : Form
{
    private readonly ProjectSpec _project;
    private readonly TextBox _id = new();
    private readonly TextBox _name = new();
    private readonly ComboBox _hot = new();
    private readonly ComboBox _env = new();
    private readonly TextBox _rate = new();
    private readonly TextBox _ts = new();
    private readonly TextBox _tt = new();
    private readonly TextBox _dp = new();
    private readonly CheckBox _allowSplit = new();
    private readonly TextBox _maxSplits = new();

    public FlowPropsDialog(ProjectSpec project, GridLine? existing)
    {
        _project = project;
        Text = existing is null ? "Новый поток" : "Правка потока";
        StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterParent;
        Width = 410;

        var table = new TableLayoutPanel { Dock = DockStyle.Top, ColumnCount = 2, AutoSize = true, Padding = new Padding(8) };
        void AddRow(string label, Control c)
        {
            table.Controls.Add(new Label { Text = label, AutoSize = true, Margin = new Padding(3, 8, 3, 0) });
            c.Width = 220;
            table.Controls.Add(c);
        }
        AddRow("Id:", _id);
        AddRow("Наименование:", _name);
        _hot.DropDownStyle = ComboBoxStyle.DropDownList;
        _hot.Items.AddRange(new object[] { "Горячий (охлаждается)", "Холодный (нагревается)" });
        _hot.SelectedIndex = 0;
        AddRow("Тип:", _hot);
        _env.DropDownStyle = ComboBoxStyle.DropDownList;
        foreach (var e in project.Environments) _env.Items.Add($"{e.Id} — {e.Name}");
        AddRow("Среда:", _env);
        AddRow("Расход, кг/ч:", _rate);
        AddRow("T нач., °C:", _ts);
        AddRow("T цель, °C:", _tt);
        AddRow("Бюджет dP, МПа:", _dp);
        _allowSplit.Text = "Разветвления разрешены (UI2b)"; _allowSplit.AutoSize = true;
        table.Controls.Add(new Label()); table.Controls.Add(_allowSplit);
        AddRow("Макс. делений:", _maxSplits);

        var ok = new Button { Text = "OK", DialogResult = DialogResult.OK, Width = 90 };
        var cancel = new Button { Text = "Отмена", DialogResult = DialogResult.Cancel, Width = 90 };
        var flow = new FlowLayoutPanel { Dock = DockStyle.Bottom, Height = 44, FlowDirection = FlowDirection.RightToLeft };
        flow.Controls.Add(ok); flow.Controls.Add(cancel);
        Controls.Add(table); Controls.Add(flow);
        AcceptButton = ok; CancelButton = cancel;
        _rate.Text = "10000"; _ts.Text = "100"; _tt.Text = "40"; _dp.Text = "0.1"; _maxSplits.Text = "0";
        if (_env.Items.Count > 0) _env.SelectedIndex = 0;
        if (existing is not null)
        {
            _id.Text = existing.Id;
            _name.Text = existing.Name;
            _hot.SelectedIndex = existing.IsHot ? 0 : 1;
            int idx = _project.Environments.ToList().FindIndex(e => e.Id == existing.EnvId);
            if (idx >= 0) _env.SelectedIndex = idx;
            _rate.Text = existing.RateKgH.ToString("0.####");
            _ts.Text = existing.TStart.ToString("0.##");
            _tt.Text = existing.TTarget.ToString("0.##");
            _dp.Text = existing.DpBudget.ToString("0.####");
            _allowSplit.Checked = existing.AllowSplit;
            _maxSplits.Text = existing.MaxSplits.ToString();
        }
    }

    public bool IsHot => _hot.SelectedIndex == 0;
    public string EnvId => _project.Environments.Count > 0 && _env.SelectedIndex >= 0 ? _project.Environments[_env.SelectedIndex].Id : "";
    public string FlowName => _name.Text;
    public double Rate => Parse(_rate.Text, 10000);
    public double TStart => Parse(_ts.Text, 100);
    public double TTarget => Parse(_tt.Text, 40);
    public double DpBudget => Parse(_dp.Text, 0.1);
    public bool AllowSplit => _allowSplit.Checked;
    public int MaxSplits => (int)Math.Max(0, Math.Round(Parse(_maxSplits.Text, 0)));

    public void WriteTo(GridLine line)
    {
        line.Name = FlowName;
        line.IsHot = IsHot;
        line.EnvId = EnvId;
        line.RateKgH = Rate;
        line.TStart = TStart;
        line.TTarget = TTarget;
        line.DpBudget = DpBudget;
        line.AllowSplit = AllowSplit;
        line.MaxSplits = MaxSplits;
    }

    public string UniqueId(HashSet<string> taken)
    {
        string id = _id.Text.Trim();
        if (id.Length == 0 || taken.Contains(id))
        {
            int n = taken.Count + 1;
            while (taken.Contains($"F{n}")) n++;
            id = $"F{n}";
        }
        return id;
    }

    private static double Parse(string s, double def) =>
        double.TryParse((s ?? "").Trim().Replace(',', '.'), System.Globalization.NumberStyles.Any,
            System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double d) ? d : def;
}








=== FILE: pinchess/src/Pinchess.App/SchemeGridResultForm.cs ===








using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Sim;

namespace Pinchess.App;

/// <summary>
/// Сетчатая диаграмма результата (UI2a): потоки — полоски по накопленному Q (X) и температуре
/// (Y — единая шкала), аппараты — кружки на горячей и холодной кривой (связаны), у кружков —
/// крайние температуры до/после из SimResult, на линиях — расход в начале/конце и по ветвям
/// делений (PathId). Смоук — у владельца.
/// </summary>
public sealed class SchemeGridResultForm : Form
{
    private readonly ProjectSpec _project;
    private readonly State _state;
    private readonly SimResult _sim;
    private readonly Panel _canvas = new();

    private sealed record Segment(string FlowId, bool IsHot, string PathId, double Q0, double Q1,
        double Tin, double Tout, double Rate, string AppId, bool IsFirst, bool IsLast);

    private readonly List<Segment> _segments = new();
    private readonly Dictionary<string, (double ThIn, double ThOut, double TcIn, double TcOut, double QMw)> _res = new();
    private readonly List<(string AppId, int Xh, int Yh, int Xc, int Yc)> _circles = new();
    private double _qMax = 1; private double _tMin, _tMax;

    public SchemeGridResultForm(ProjectSpec project, State state, SimResult sim)
    {
        _project = project;
        _state = state;
        _sim = sim;
        Text = "Сетчатая диаграмма — результат (UI2a)";
        StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterParent;
        Size = new Size(1180, 720);
        MinimumSize = new Size(900, 560);

        foreach (var r in sim.Apparatus)
            _res[r.ApparatusId] = (r.ThInC, r.ThOutC, r.TcInC, r.TcOutC, r.QMw);

        BuildSegments();

        var tools = new FlowLayoutPanel { Dock = DockStyle.Top, Height = 44, Padding = new Padding(8, 6, 8, 0) };
        tools.Controls.Add(new Label
        {
            Text = $"Q итого: {_sim.QTotalMw:0.000} МВт · сходимость {(sim.AllConverged ? "да" : "нет")} · " +
                   $"горячие — красные, холодные — синие; кружок: T до→после",
            AutoSize = true, ForeColor = Color.DimGray, Margin = new Padding(4, 8, 0, 0),
        });

        _canvas.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
        _canvas.BackColor = Color.White;
        _canvas.Paint += CanvasPaint;

        Controls.Add(_canvas);
        Controls.Add(tools);
    }

    private void BuildSegments()
    {
        foreach (var flow in _project.Flows)
        {
            Side side = flow.IsHot ? Side.Hot : Side.Cold;
            var paths = _state.Committed
                .Where(i => i.FlowId == flow.Id && i.Side == side)
                .GroupBy(i => flow.IsHot ? i.HotPathId : i.ColdPathId)
                .ToList();
            foreach (var path in paths)
            {
                double fraction = path.First().SplitFractionA is > 0 and < 1 || path.Any(x => x.SplitFractionA > 0 && x.SplitFractionA < 1)
                    ? path.Max(x => x.SplitFractionA) : 1.0;
                double rate = flow.FlowRateKgH * fraction;
                var chain = path.Where(i => !i.Bypass).OrderBy(i => i.PositionIndex).ToList();
                double cumQ = 0;
                double tIn = flow.TStartC;
                for (int k = 0; k < chain.Count; k++)
                {
                    var inst = chain[k];
                    if (!_res.TryGetValue(inst.ApparatusId, out var r)) continue;
                    double tin = flow.IsHot ? r.ThIn : r.TcIn;
                    double tout = flow.IsHot ? r.ThOut : r.TcOut;
                    if (k == 0) tIn = tin;
                    _segments.Add(new Segment(flow.Id, flow.IsHot, path.Key, cumQ, cumQ + Math.Abs(r.QMw),
                        tin, tout, rate, inst.ApparatusId, k == 0, k == chain.Count - 1));
                    cumQ += Math.Abs(r.QMw);
                }
            }
        }
        _qMax = Math.Max(1e-6, _segments.Count == 0 ? 1 : _segments.Max(s => s.Q1));
        var temps = new List<double>();
        foreach (var f in _project.Flows) { temps.Add(f.TStartC); temps.Add(f.TTargetC); }
        foreach (var s in _segments) { temps.Add(s.Tin); temps.Add(s.Tout); }
        _tMin = temps.Count > 0 ? temps.Min() - 15 : 0;
        _tMax = temps.Count > 0 ? temps.Max() + 15 : 100;
    }

    private int Px(double q, int plotW) => 70 + (int)(q / _qMax * Math.Max(200, plotW));
    private int Py(double t, int plotH) => 30 + (int)((_tMax - t) / Math.Max(1, _tMax - _tMin) * Math.Max(200, plotH));

    private void CanvasPaint(object? sender, PaintEventArgs e)
    {
        var g = e.Graphics;
        int w = _canvas.Width - 100, h = _canvas.Height - 60;
        if (w < 200 || h < 200) return;
        g.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
        using var gridPen = new Pen(Color.Gainsboro, 1f);
        for (int i = 0; i <= 10; i++)
        {
            int x = 70 + i * w / 10;
            g.DrawLine(gridPen, x, 30, x, 30 + h);
        }
        for (int i = 0; i <= 10; i++)
        {
            int y = 30 + i * h / 10;
            g.DrawLine(gridPen, 70, y, 70 + w, y);
            double t = _tMax - (_tMax - _tMin) * i / 10.0;
            g.DrawString($"{t:0}", Control.DefaultFont, Brushes.DimGray, 30, y - 7);
        }
        g.DrawString("Q →", Control.DefaultFont, Brushes.DimGray, 70 + w / 2, 8);

        // кружки аппаратов: позиция по Q и T каждой стороны
        _circles.Clear();
        foreach (var grp in _segments.GroupBy(s => s.AppId))
        {
            var hotSeg = grp.FirstOrDefault(s => s.IsHot);
            var coldSeg = grp.FirstOrDefault(s => !s.IsHot);
            if (hotSeg is { } hs)
            {
                var c = _res[hs.AppId];
                _circles.Add((hs.AppId, Px((hs.Q0 + hs.Q1) / 2, w), Py((c.ThIn + c.ThOut) / 2, h), 0, 0));
            }
            if (coldSeg is { } cs)
            {
                var c = _res[cs.AppId];
                _circles.Add((cs.AppId, 0, 0, Px((cs.Q0 + cs.Q1) / 2, w), Py((c.TcIn + c.TcOut) / 2, h)));
            }
        }
        var byApp = _circles.GroupBy(c => c.AppId).ToDictionary(k => k.Key, v => v.ToList());

        // линии-сегменты по (FlowId, PathId)
        foreach (var grp in _segments.GroupBy(s => (s.FlowId, s.PathId)))
        {
            var list = grp.OrderBy(s => s.Q0).ToList();
            foreach (var seg in list)
            {
                var pen = new Pen(seg.IsHot ? Color.Firebrick : Color.RoyalBlue, seg.IsHot ? 4f : 3.5f);
                if (seg.IsLast) pen.EndCap = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.LineCap.ArrowAnchor;
                int x0 = Px(seg.Q0, w), x1 = Px(seg.Q1, w);
                int y0 = Py(seg.Tin, h), y1 = Py(seg.Tout, h);
                if (Math.Abs(x1 - x0) < 2) x1 = x0 + 2;
                g.DrawLine(pen, x0, y0, x1, y1);
                pen.Dispose();
                // расход: начало линии и концы (по T-порядку не важно — подпись у первого сегмента)
                if (seg.IsFirst)
                {
                    SchemeGridForm.DrawLabel(g, x0 - 4, y0 - 20, $"{seg.FlowId}: G = {seg.Rate:0.#} кг/ч",
                        seg.IsHot ? Color.Firebrick : Color.RoyalBlue);
                }
                if (seg.IsLast)
                {
                    SchemeGridForm.DrawLabel(g, x1, y1 + 8, $"{seg.Tout:0.#} °C", Color.DimGray);
                }
                if (_res.TryGetValue(seg.AppId, out var r))
                {
                    string label = seg.IsHot ? $"Th {r.ThIn:0.#}→{r.ThOut:0.#}" : $"Tc {r.TcIn:0.#}→{r.TcOut:0.#}";
                    SchemeGridForm.DrawLabel(g, x1 - 20, seg.IsHot ? y1 - 34 : y1 + 12, label,
                        seg.IsHot ? Color.Firebrick : Color.RoyalBlue);
                }
            }
        }

        // кружки и связки аппаратов
        foreach (var (appId, list) in byApp)
        {
            var hotC = list.FirstOrDefault(c => c.Yh > 0);
            var coldC = list.FirstOrDefault(c => c.Xc > 0);
            if (hotC.Xh > 0 && coldC.Xc > 0)
            {
                using var link = new Pen(Color.SeaGreen, 1.6f) { DashStyle = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.DashStyle.Dot };
                g.DrawLine(link, hotC.Xh, hotC.Yh, coldC.Xc, coldC.Yc);
            }
            if (hotC.Xh > 0) DrawCircle(g, hotC.Xh, hotC.Yh, Color.Firebrick);
            if (coldC.Xc > 0) DrawCircle(g, coldC.Xc, coldC.Yc, Color.RoyalBlue);
            if (hotC.Xh > 0)
                SchemeGridForm.DrawLabel(g, hotC.Xh + 8, hotC.Yh - 26, appId, Color.Firebrick);
        }
    }

    private static void DrawCircle(Graphics g, int x, int y, Color color)
    {
        using var fill = new SolidBrush(Color.FromArgb(200, color));
        g.FillEllipse(fill, x - 7, y - 7, 14, 14);
        g.FillEllipse(Brushes.White, x - 3, y - 3, 6, 6);
    }
}








=== FILE: pinchess/src/Pinchess.App/StatsForm.cs ===








using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Scheme;
using Pinchess.Engine.Search;
using Pinchess.Engine.Sim;
using Pinchess.Engine.Stats;

namespace Pinchess.App;

/// <summary>
/// S11: окно подробной статистики топ-10: аппараты с температурами, K0→K', Re, скоростями;
/// «сгоревший градиент» столбиком; Q_рек/Q_glob; чувствительность K (Q при K0/2 и K0·2);
/// связь со схемой — кнопка «Схема» с подсветкой выбранного аппарата.
/// </summary>
public sealed class StatsForm : Form
{
    private readonly ProjectSpec _project;
    private readonly SearchOutcome _outcome;
    private readonly ListBox _list = new() { Dock = DockStyle.Left, Width = 320 };
    private readonly DataGridView _grid = new()
    {
        Dock = DockStyle.Fill, ReadOnly = true, AutoSizeColumnsMode = DataGridViewAutoSizeColumnsMode.Fill,
        AllowUserToAddRows = false,
    };
    private readonly Panel _burntPanel = new() { Dock = DockStyle.Bottom, Height = 130, BackColor = Color.White };
    private readonly Label _labelSens = new() { Dock = DockStyle.Bottom, Height = 48, Text = "", Padding = new Padding(6) };
    private readonly Label _labelTotal = new() { Dock = DockStyle.Bottom, Height = 28, Text = "", Padding = new Padding(6) };
    private List<AppStatsRow> _currentRows = new();

    public StatsForm(ProjectSpec project, SearchOutcome outcome)
    {
        _project = project;
        _outcome = outcome;
        Text = "Статистика топ-10 (S11)";
        StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterParent;
        Size = new Size(1160, 700);

        var top = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Top, Height = 36, Padding = new Padding(4) };
        var btnScheme = new Button { Text = "Схема (подсветить выбранный)", Width = 220, Left = 4, Top = 4 };
        btnScheme.Click += (s, e) => ShowScheme();
        top.Controls.Add(btnScheme);

        _burntPanel.Paint += (s, e) => DrawBurnt(e.Graphics, e.ClipRectangle);

        Controls.Add(_grid);
        Controls.Add(_burntPanel);
        Controls.Add(_labelSens);
        Controls.Add(_labelTotal);
        Controls.Add(_list);
        Controls.Add(top);
        _labelTotal.BringToFront();
        _labelSens.BringToFront();
        _burntPanel.BringToFront();
        _grid.BringToFront();

        var top10 = outcome.Top10 ?? new List<(State, double)>();
        for (int i = 0; i < top10.Count; i++)
            _list.Items.Add($"#{i + 1} · Q = {top10[i].QMw:0.000} МВт · М1 = {top10[i].State.MountChanges}");
        if (top10.Count == 0)
        {
            _list.Items.Add("Топ-10 пуст (решений не найдено)");
            return;
        }
        _list.SelectedIndexChanged += (s, e) => ShowVariant();
        _grid.CellClick += (s, e) => _burntPanel.Invalidate();
        _list.SelectedIndex = 0;

        double qGlob = Math.Max(project.QglobMw, 1e-9);
        _labelTotal.Text = $"Q_рек/Q_glob = {outcome.QBestMw / qGlob * 100:0.0} %  ·  «сгоревший градиент» — оранжевые полосы (потерянный напор по аппаратам)";
    }

    private void ShowVariant()
    {
        var top10 = _outcome.Top10 ?? new List<(State, double)>();
        if (_list.SelectedIndex < 0 || _list.SelectedIndex >= top10.Count) return;
        var (state, q) = top10[_list.SelectedIndex];
        var sim = new Simulator(_project);
        var res = sim.Simulate(state, SimLevel.Fine);
        _currentRows = StatsService.Rows(_project, state, res).ToList();

        _grid.Columns.Clear();
        _grid.Rows.Clear();
        foreach (var (hdr, w) in new[]
        {
            ("Аппарат", 70), ("Горячий поток", 100), ("Th в→выход", 110), ("Холодный поток", 110),
            ("Tc в→выход", 110), ("Q, МВт", 70), ("Сгор. градиент", 100), ("A, м²", 60),
            ("K0", 50), ("K'", 60), ("Re гор", 70), ("Re хол", 70), ("v гор, м/с", 80), ("v хол, м/с", 80), ("Статус", 90)
        })
            _grid.Columns[_grid.Columns.Add(hdr, hdr)].Width = w;

        foreach (var r in _currentRows)
        {
            _grid.Rows.Add(
                r.ApparatusId,
                r.HotFlowId ?? "—",
                r.ThInC is null ? "—" : $"{r.ThInC:0.0}→{r.ThOutC:0.0}",
                r.ColdFlowId ?? "—",
                r.TcInC is null ? "—" : $"{r.TcInC:0.0}→{r.TcOutC:0.0}",
                r.QMw is null ? "—" : $"{r.QMw:0.000}",
                $"{r.BurntMw:0.000}",
                $"{r.AreaM2:0}",
                $"{r.K0Wm2K:0}",
                r.KFineWm2K is null ? "—" : $"{r.KFineWm2K:0}",
                r.ReHot is null ? "—" : $"{r.ReHot:0}",
                r.ReCold is null ? "—" : $"{r.ReCold:0}",
                r.VHotMs is null ? "—" : $"{r.VHotMs:0.00}",
                r.VColdMs is null ? "—" : $"{r.VColdMs:0.00}",
                r.FailureClass ?? "OK");
        }

        var sens = StatsService.SensitivityK(_project, new[] { (state, q) }, 0.5, 2.0)[0];
        string fmt(double v) => double.IsNegativeInfinity(v) ? "не сошлось" : $"{v:0.000} МВт";
        _labelSens.Text = $"Чувствительность K (вариант #{_list.SelectedIndex + 1}): Q(K0/2) = {fmt(sens.QKMinMw)} · Q(K0·2) = {fmt(sens.QKMaxMw)} — диапазон полной физики, Э1-калибровка уточнит";
        _burntPanel.Invalidate();
    }

    private void DrawBurnt(Graphics g, Rectangle clip)
    {
        g.Clear(Color.White);
        if (_currentRows.Count == 0) return;
        double max = _currentRows.Max(r => r.BurntMw);
        if (max < 1e-9) max = 1;
        float barH = 16;
        float y = 4;
        foreach (var r in _currentRows)
        {
            float w = (float)(r.BurntMw / max * (clip.Width - 150));
            using var brush = new SolidBrush(Color.FromArgb(230, 140, 0));
            g.FillRectangle(brush, 130, y, Math.Max(1, w), barH);
            g.DrawString($"{r.ApparatusId}  {r.BurntMw:0.000} МВт", Font, Brushes.Black, 4, y);
            y += barH + 4;
            if (y + barH > clip.Height) break;
        }
    }

    private void ShowScheme()
    {
        var top10 = _outcome.Top10 ?? new List<(State, double)>();
        if (_list.SelectedIndex < 0 || _list.SelectedIndex >= top10.Count) return;
        var (state, _) = top10[_list.SelectedIndex];
        string? highlight = null;
        if (_grid.CurrentCell is { } cell && cell.RowIndex >= 0 && cell.RowIndex < _currentRows.Count)
            highlight = _currentRows[cell.RowIndex].ApparatusId;
        var sim = new Simulator(_project);
        var res = sim.Simulate(state, SimLevel.Coarse);
        using var form = new SchemeForm(_project, state, res, null,
            $"Схема варианта #{_list.SelectedIndex + 1}");
        form.SetHighlight(highlight);
        form.ShowDialog(this);
    }
}








=== FILE: pinchess/src/Pinchess.App/Theme.cs ===




namespace Pinchess.App;

using System.Drawing;

/// <summary>
/// UI-пакет (решение владельца 21:45): токены темы (тёмная/светлая) — центральная замена
/// жёстко зашитых цветов. Apply рекурсивно красит форму и её деревья контролов.
/// </summary>
public static class Theme
{
    public static bool Dark;

    public static Color Back => Dark ? Color.FromArgb(30, 31, 35) : SystemColors.Control;
    public static Color Panel => Dark ? Color.FromArgb(42, 43, 48) : SystemColors.ControlLight;
    public static Color Text => Dark ? Color.Gainsboro : SystemColors.ControlText;
    public static Color Accent => Dark ? Color.FromArgb(96, 170, 255) : Color.RoyalBlue;
    public static Color Border => Dark ? Color.FromArgb(64, 65, 72) : SystemColors.ControlDark;

    public static void Apply(Form form)
    {
        form.BackColor = Back;
        form.ForeColor = Text;
        ApplyChildren(form.Controls);
    }

    private static void ApplyChildren(Control.ControlCollection controls)
    {
        foreach (Control c in controls)
        {
            if (c is Button || c is ToolStrip)
            {
                ApplyChildren(c.Controls);
                continue; // кнопки/меню — системный рендер, не перекрашиваем
            }
            c.BackColor = Panel;
            c.ForeColor = Text;
            ApplyChildren(c.Controls);
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: pinchess/src/Pinchess.Cli/Program.cs ===








namespace Pinchess.Cli;

using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Io;
using Pinchess.Engine.Search;
using Pinchess.Engine.Sim;

/// <summary>
/// S13: CLI-режим движка для ночных прогонов: воспроизводит GUI-результат при тех же параметрах.
/// Пример: Pinchess.Cli --project project.json --mode beam --beam 200 --max-states 200000
///         --checkpoint-every 5000 --checkpoint-path ck.json --out result.json --pdf report.pdf
/// </summary>
public static class Program
{
    public static int Main(string[] args)
    {
        try
        {
            string? projectPath = null, outPath = null, pdfPath = null, ckPath = null, resumePath = null;
            string mode = "beam";
            int beamW = 200;
            long maxStates = 200_000;
            long ckEvery = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < args.Length; i++)
            {
                string Next() => args[++i];
                switch (args[i])
                {
                    case "--project": projectPath = Next(); break;
                    case "--mode": mode = Next(); break;
                    case "--beam": beamW = int.Parse(Next()); break;
                    case "--max-states": maxStates = long.Parse(Next()); break;
                    case "--checkpoint-every": ckEvery = long.Parse(Next()); break;
                    case "--checkpoint-path": ckPath = Next(); break;
                    case "--resume": resumePath = Next(); break;
                    case "--out": outPath = Next(); break;
                    case "--pdf": pdfPath = Next(); break;
                    default: Console.Error.WriteLine($"Неизвестный аргумент: {args[i]}"); return 2;
                }
            }
            if (projectPath is null)
            {
                Console.Error.WriteLine("Обязателен --project <path.json>");
                return 2;
            }

            var (project, report) = ProjectJsonIO.Load(projectPath);
            if (project is null)
            {
                Console.Error.WriteLine("Проект не загрузился. Ошибки:");
                Console.Error.WriteLine(report.ToString());
                return 3;
            }

            var kind = mode.Equals("strict", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? SearchModeKind.StrictBnB : SearchModeKind.BeamSearch;
            var run = new SearchRun(kind, beamW, maxStates, null, null, CancellationToken.None,
                CheckpointEveryStates: ckEvery > 0 ? ckEvery : null,
                CheckpointPath: ckPath);

            SearchEngine engine;
            if (resumePath is not null)
            {
                var dto = SearchCheckpoint.FromJson(File.ReadAllText(resumePath));
                var frontier = dto.Frontier.Select(f => (SearchCheckpoint.FromDto(f, project), f.Ub)).ToList();
                engine = new SearchEngine(project, run, frontier, dto.QBestMw,
                    dto.BestState is null ? null : SearchCheckpoint.FromDto(dto.BestState, project));
                Console.WriteLine($"Возобновление скана: frontier={frontier.Count}, incumbent={dto.QBestMw:0.000} МВт");
            }
            else
            {
                engine = new SearchEngine(project, run);
            }

            var outcome = engine.Run(p => Console.WriteLine($"eval={p.StatesEvaluated} frontier={p.Frontier} Q_best={(double.IsNegativeInfinity(p.QBestMw) ? "-inf" : p.QBestMw.ToString("0.000"))}"));
            var cert = outcome.Certificate;
            Console.WriteLine(cert.ToString());
            if (outcome.Report is not null)
            {
                Console.WriteLine(outcome.Report.ToString());
            }

            if (outPath is not null)
            {
                ScanResultIO.Save(ScanResultIO.FromOutcome(project, outcome), outPath);
                Console.WriteLine($"Результат сохранён: {outPath}");
            }
            if (pdfPath is not null && outcome.Best is { } best)
            {
                var sim = new Simulator(project);
                var res = sim.Simulate(best, SimLevel.Fine);
                Pinchess.Reports.PdfReport.Build(project, best, res, outcome, null, pdfPath);
                Console.WriteLine($"PDF-отчёт: {pdfPath}");
            }
            return 0;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Console.Error.WriteLine("Ошибка CLI: " + ex.Message);
            return 1;
        }
    }
}
