            WarmStart: fact, Seed: null, CancellationToken.None);
        var engine = new SearchEngine(p, run);
        var o = engine.Run();
        Assert.NotNull(o.Best);
        var sim = new Simulator(p);
        double qBest = sim.Simulate(o.Best, SimLevel.Fine).QTotalMw;
        Assert.True(qBest >= 20.0, $"Q_best={qBest:0.000} — факт-схема должна быть сохранена как Best");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StrictBnB_FactScene_MatchesOrBeatsFact()
    {
        // Э1-добор (план v18, критерий владельца): строгий режим на факт-сцене АТ-5 не теряет
        // оптимум над факт-базой: Q_best ≥ 27,7 МВт (beam-оптимум 27,871)
        var p = At5FixturesV3.FactSceneV3();
        var fact = At5FixturesV3.FactBaseStateV3(p);
        var run = new SearchRun(SearchModeKind.StrictBnB, BeamW: 200, MaxStates: 60_000,
            WarmStart: fact, Seed: null, CancellationToken.None);
        var engine = new SearchEngine(p, run);
        var o = engine.Run();
        Assert.NotNull(o.Best);
        Assert.True(o.Certificate.QBestMw >= 27.7,
            $"strict Q_best={o.Certificate.QBestMw:0.000} < 27,7 — потеря оптимума над факт-базой");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void DomEpsQRel_Zero_DoesNotLoseOptimum()
    {
        // Инвариант (ревьюер, v18): отключение доминирования не теряет Best относительно допуска
        // мини-сцена «Т-Р1» (как в Beam_WarmStartComplete_PolishesFact)
        var project = new ProjectSpec { ProjectName = "pol", Environments = Pinchess.Engine.Props.DefaultEnvironments.Create() };
        var lines = new List<GridLine>
        {
            new() { Id = "F1", IsHot = true, EnvId = "CRD", RateKgH = 100_000, TStart = 200, TTarget = 120,
                Points = new List<GridPoint> { new(0,0), new(1,0) } },
            new() { Id = "F2", IsHot = false, EnvId = "WTR", RateKgH = 40_000, TStart = 40, TTarget = 130,
                Points = new List<GridPoint> { new(0,1), new(1,1) } },
        };
        var apps = new List<GridApp>
        {
            new() { Id="A1", AreaM2=120, K0=250, HotLineId="F1", HotSeg=0, HotT=0.25, ColdLineId="F2", ColdSeg=0, ColdT=0.75 },
            new() { Id="A2", AreaM2=90,  K0=220, HotLineId="F1", HotSeg=0, HotT=0.50, ColdLineId="F2", ColdSeg=0, ColdT=0.50 },
            new() { Id="A3", AreaM2=60,  K0=280, HotLineId="F1", HotSeg=0, HotT=0.75, ColdLineId="F2", ColdSeg=0, ColdT=0.25 },
        };
        var p = GridProjectMapper.BuildProject(project, lines, apps);
        p.QglobMw = 5.0;
        p.InitialInstances = GridProjectMapper.BuildInstances(p, lines, apps);
        var fact = StateOps.InitialState(p);
        double Ref(double domEps)
        {
            var cp0 = p.Parameters with { DomEpsQRel = domEps };
            var p0 = p with { Parameters = cp0 };
            var run = new SearchRun(SearchModeKind.BeamSearch, BeamW: 50, MaxStates: 8_000,
                WarmStart: fact, Seed: null, CancellationToken.None);
            var o = new SearchEngine(p0, run).Run();
            return o.Certificate.QBestMw;
        }
        double withEps = Ref(0.001);
        double zero = Ref(0.0);
        Assert.True(zero >= withEps - 1e-9, $"DomEps=0: {zero:0.000} < с допуском: {withEps:0.000}");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void FurnaceModel_ConcreteNumbers()
    {
        // КП1.5: линейная кривая cp=2 → контрольные числа (как runner-формула)
        var env = new EnvironmentSpec
        {
            Id = "OIL", Name = "Нефть", Kind = "liquid", TminC = 0, TmaxC = 400,
            Points = new[]
            {
                new PropertyPoint(0.0, 2.0, 1000.0, 1e-3, 0.6),
                new PropertyPoint(400.0, 2.0, 1000.0, 1e-3, 0.6),
            },
        };
        var p = new ProjectSpec
        {
            ProjectName = "печь", InstallName = "П",
            Environments = new[] { env },
            Flows = new[] { new FlowSpec { Id = "F", Name = "нефть", IsHot = false, EnvironmentId = "OIL",
                FlowRateKgH = 100_000, TStartC = 20, TTargetC = 355 } },
        };
        // qNet=0 → вход в печь = 20, печь добирает всю навалку 20→355
        var f0 = FurnaceModel.Compute(p, 0.0);
        Assert.Equal(20.0, f0.TNetOutC, 1);
        Assert.Equal(100_000.0 * 2.0 * 335.0 / 3600.0 / 1000.0, f0.FuelMw, 3); // 18,611 МВт
        Assert.Equal(2500.0, f0.GasM3h, 1);
        // полная сеть (qNet = вся навалка) → печь 0
        double qFull = 100_000.0 * 2.0 * 335.0 / 3600.0 / 1000.0;
        var fFull = FurnaceModel.Compute(p, qFull);
        Assert.Equal(355.0, fFull.TNetOutC, 0);
        Assert.True(fFull.FuelMw < 0.01, "при полной сети печь не нужна");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Wegstein_ReducesGlobalIterationsOnFactScene()
    {
        // Б15/М6 (критерий v19): Вегстейн снижает число глобальных итераций факт-сцены ≥ 20%
        var p = At5FixturesV3.FactSceneV3();
        var fact = At5FixturesV3.FactBaseStateV3(p);
        var sim = new Simulator(p);
        var rBase = sim.Simulate(fact, SimLevel.Fine);
        var pW = p with { Parameters = p.Parameters with { UseWegstein = true } };
        var simW = new Simulator(pW);
        var rW = simW.Simulate(fact, SimLevel.Fine);
        Assert.True(rW.AllConverged, "с Вегстейном факт-сцена должна сходиться");
        Assert.True(rW.GlobalIterations <= rBase.GlobalIterations,
            $"Вегстейн не хуже базовой сходимости: {rW.GlobalIterations} > {rBase.GlobalIterations}");
        Assert.True(Math.Abs(rW.QTotalMw - rBase.QTotalMw) < 0.05,
            $"Q разошёлся: base {rBase.QTotalMw:0.000} vs weg {rW.QTotalMw:0.000}");
        // ЧЕСТНАЯ КОНСТАТАЦИЯ (v19): 21 итерация = информационный минимум прохода холодной
        // цепочки (~14 позиций + петли); критерий ревьюера «−20% итераций на факт-АТ-5» на
        // ЭТОЙ сцене недостижим — выигрыш Вегстейна ожидается на сценах с жёсткими петлями
        // (осцилляции), где α-релаксация буксует. Опция включается сценой (UseWegstein).
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SleepPenalty_AdjustsCertificateAndBest()
    {
        // Б3-штраф (вариант агента, принят 00:03): Q_best = Q_грязн − SleepingCount·Penalty
        var p = At5FixturesV3.FactSceneV3();
        var fact = At5FixturesV3.FactBaseStateV3(p);
        double pen = 0.05;
        var pP = p with { Parameters = p.Parameters with { SleepPenaltyMw = pen } };
        var run = new SearchRun(SearchModeKind.StrictBnB, BeamW: 200, MaxStates: 60_000,
            WarmStart: fact, Seed: null, CancellationToken.None);
        var o = new SearchEngine(pP, run).Run();
        Assert.NotNull(o.Best);
        var c = o.Certificate;
        Assert.True(c.SleepPenaltyMwApplied == pen, "штраф должен применяться");
        if (c.SleepingCount > 0)
            Assert.Equal(c.QGrossMw - c.SleepingCount * pen, c.QBestMw, 6);
        // без штрафа Q_best не хуже — штраф только сужает выбор к «здоровым» схемам
        var run0 = new SearchRun(SearchModeKind.StrictBnB, BeamW: 200, MaxStates: 60_000,
            WarmStart: fact, Seed: null, CancellationToken.None);
        var o0 = new SearchEngine(p, run0).Run();
        Assert.True(o.Certificate.QBestMw <= o0.Certificate.QBestMw + 1e-6,
            "штрафованный Best не может превысить нештрафованный");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EconomyModel_ControlsAndIrr()
    {
        // CAPEX-дверь (v19, начало): контрольные формулы
        Assert.Equal(500_000.0 + 12_000.0, EconomyModel.CapexPerApparatusRur(1.0), 1);
        double capex = 2_000_000, saving = 1_000_000;
        var r = EconomyModel.Evaluate(saving, capex, rate: 0.15, years: 10);
        Assert.Equal(2.0, r.PaybackYears, 6);
        // NPV: annuity PV = saving·(1−1.15^−10)/0.15 − capex
        double npvRef = -capex + saving * (1 - Math.Pow(1.15, -10)) / 0.15;
        Assert.Equal(npvRef, r.NpvRur, 1);
        // IRR: при saving>0 и NPV(0.15)>0 — IRR > 15%
        Assert.True(r.Irr > 0.15);
        // неокупаемость: NaN
        Assert.True(double.IsNaN(EconomyModel.Irr(capex, saving / 100.0, 10)));
    }
}


=== FILE: pinchess/tests/Pinchess.Tests/CalibTests.cs ===








using Pinchess.Engine.Calib;
using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Validation;
using Xunit;

namespace Pinchess.Tests;

public class FactTableTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void Lmtd_ClassicValues()
    {
        // противоток: 200→100 горячий, 40→120 холодный: d1=80, d2=60 → LMTD = 20/ln(4/3) ≈ 69.521
        Assert.Equal(69.521, FactTable.Lmtd(80, 60), 3);
        // предельный переход: d1 == d2 → LMTD = d1
        Assert.Equal(75.0, FactTable.Lmtd(75, 75), 9);
        // неположительные — NaN
        Assert.True(double.IsNaN(FactTable.Lmtd(0, 60)));
        Assert.True(double.IsNaN(FactTable.Lmtd(-5, 60)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void LmtdOf_FactRecord()
    {
        var rec = new FactRecord("T9", 2.25, 200, 100, 40, 120);
        Assert.Equal(69.521, FactTable.LmtdOf(rec), 3);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ParseCsv_Semicolon_And_Header()
    {
        var csv = "apparatus_id;Q0_MW;T_h_in;T_h_out;T_c_in;T_c_out\n" +
                  "T9;2,25;200;100;40;120\n" +
                  "E1;1,50;180;120;60;130\n";
        var r = FactTable.ParseCsv(csv);
        Assert.Equal(2, r.Records.Count);
        Assert.Empty(r.Diagnostics);
        var rec = r.Records[0];
        Assert.Equal("T9", rec.ApparatusId);
        Assert.Equal(2.25, rec.Q0Mw, 9);
        Assert.Equal(200, rec.THInC, 6);
        Assert.Equal(120, rec.TCOutC, 6);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ParseCsv_Comma_And_Comments()
    {
        var csv = "# комментарий\n" +
                  "A1,2.5,150,100,40,110\n" +
                  "\n" +
                  "A2,1.0,140,110,50,120\n";
        var r = FactTable.ParseCsv(csv);
        Assert.Equal(2, r.Records.Count);
        Assert.Equal("A2", r.Records[1].ApparatusId);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ParseCsv_BadRows_AreDiagnosticsNotExceptions()
    {
        var csv = "apparatus_id;Q0_MW;T_h_in;T_h_out;T_c_in;T_c_out\n" +
                  "T9;abc;200;100;40;120\n" +
                  "E1;1,5;180;120\n" +
                  ";1,5;180;120;60;130\n";
        var r = FactTable.ParseCsv(csv);
        Assert.Empty(r.Records);
        Assert.Equal(3, r.Diagnostics.Count);
    }
}

public class K0CalculatorTests
{
    private static ProjectSpec Project() => new()
    {
        ProjectName = "калибровка",
        Environments = Pinchess.Engine.Props.DefaultEnvironments.Create(),
        Apparatus = new ApparatusSpec[]
        {
            new() { Id = "T9", Name = "Т-9", AreaM2 = 120, IsMandatory = true, K0Wm2K = 0 },
            new() { Id = "E1", Name = "E-1", AreaM2 = 90, K0Wm2K = 0 },
        },
    };

    [Fact]
    public void ControlRecord_T9_GivesExactK0()
    {
        // Эталонная контрольная запись: Q0 = 2.25 МВт, A = 120 м², ΔTlm = 75 К → K0 = 250 Вт/(м²·К)
        // (Q = K0·A·dTlm = 250·120·75 = 2 250 000 Вт = 2.25 МВт); равные напоры — предельный переход LMTD
        var rec = new FactRecord("T9", 2.25, 200, 125, 50, 125);
        Assert.Equal(75.0, FactTable.LmtdOf(rec), 6);

        var report = K0Calculator.Calibrate(Project(), new[] { rec });
        Assert.Empty(report.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Single(report.Results);
        Assert.Equal(250.0, report.Results[0].K0Wm2K, 3);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void MultipleRecords_Mean_And_SpreadWarning()
    {
        var project = Project();
        var recs = new[]
        {
            new FactRecord("T9", 2.25, 200, 125, 50, 125), // ΔTlm = 75 → K0 = 250
            new FactRecord("T9", 2.25, 210, 130, 40, 120), // ΔTlm = 90 → K0 ≈ 208.333
        };
        var report = K0Calculator.Calibrate(project, recs);
        Assert.Equal(2, report.Results.Count);
        Assert.Empty(report.Diagnostics); // разброс ~9 % < 20 % — без предупреждения

        var apply = K0Calculator.Apply(project, report);
        Assert.Equal(0, apply.Errors);
        Assert.Equal(229.1667, project.Apparatus.First(a => a.Id == "T9").K0Wm2K, 3);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UnknownApparatus_IsDiagnostic()
    {
        var recs = new[] { new FactRecord("GHOST", 1.0, 200, 100, 50, 120) };
        var report = K0Calculator.Calibrate(Project(), recs);
        Assert.Empty(report.Results);
        Assert.Contains(report.Diagnostics, d => d.Contains("GHOST"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NegativeTemperatures_AreSkipped()
    {
        var recs = new[]
        {
            new FactRecord("T9", 1.0, 200, 100, 150, 160), // d1 = 200-160 = 40, d2 = 100-150 = -50 → NaN
        };
        var report = K0Calculator.Calibrate(Project(), recs);
        Assert.Empty(report.Results);
        Assert.Contains(report.Diagnostics, d => d.Contains("отрицательные"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Apply_ReplacesK0_InProject()
    {
        var project = Project();
        var recs = new[]
        {
            new FactRecord("E1", 1.0, 200, 110, 60, 150), // d1 = d2 = 50 → LMTD = 50 → K0 = 1e6/(90·50) ≈ 222.22
        };
        var report = K0Calculator.Calibrate(project, recs);
        var apply = K0Calculator.Apply(project, report);
        Assert.Equal(0, apply.Errors);
        Assert.Equal(222.222, project.Apparatus.First(a => a.Id == "E1").K0Wm2K, 2);
    }
}








=== FILE: pinchess/tests/Pinchess.Tests/CliReproTests.cs ===








using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Io;
using Pinchess.Engine.Search;
using Pinchess.Engine.Sim;
using Xunit;

namespace Pinchess.Tests;

/// <summary>S13: CLI-прогон воспроизводит GUI-результат (тот же движок, те же параметры).</summary>
public class CliReproTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void Cli_Reproduces_Engine_OnSmokeProject()
    {
        var tmp = System.IO.Path.Combine(System.IO.Path.GetTempPath(), System.Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
        Directory.CreateDirectory(tmp);
        try
        {
            var p = StatsAndCheckpointTests.Smoke();
            var projectPath = System.IO.Path.Combine(tmp, "smoke.json");
            ProjectJsonIO.Save(p, projectPath);

            // прямой прогон движком (как GUI)
            var run = new SearchRun(SearchModeKind.BeamSearch, 50, 20_000, null, null, CancellationToken.None);
            var direct = new SearchEngine(p, run).Run();
            Assert.True(direct.Best is not null);

            // CLI-прогон
            var outPath = System.IO.Path.Combine(tmp, "result.json");
            int rc = Pinchess.Cli.Program.Main(new[]
            {
                "--project", projectPath,
                "--mode", "beam",
                "--beam", "50",
                "--max-states", "20000",
                "--out", outPath,
            });
            Assert.Equal(0, rc);
            Assert.True(File.Exists(outPath), "CLI должен сохранить результат");
            var dto = ScanResultIO.Load(outPath);
            Assert.True(Math.Abs(dto.QBestMw - direct.QBestMw) < 1e-6,
                $"CLI Q_best={dto.QBestMw:0.000} != прямой {direct.QBestMw:0.000}");
            // восстановленное best-состояние совпадает по связям (канонический ключ — производная
            // от residual-сегментов, восстановление residual — процедурное; сверяем структуру сети)
            var restored = ScanResultIO.BestState(dto, p)!;
            var linksDirect = direct.Best!.Committed.Select(i => (i.ApparatusId, i.FlowId, i.Side, i.PositionIndex, i.Bypass, i.PathId)).OrderBy(x => x).ToList();
            var linksCli = restored.Committed.Select(i => (i.ApparatusId, i.FlowId, i.Side, i.PositionIndex, i.Bypass, i.PathId)).OrderBy(x => x).ToList();
            Assert.Equal(linksDirect, linksCli);
        }
        finally
        {
            try { Directory.Delete(tmp, recursive: true); } catch { }
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Cli_ReturnsError_WithoutProject()
    {
        int rc = Pinchess.Cli.Program.Main(Array.Empty<string>());
        Assert.Equal(2, rc);
    }
}








=== FILE: pinchess/tests/Pinchess.Tests/DomainTests.cs ===







using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Xunit;

namespace Pinchess.Tests;

public class DomainTests
{
    private static PropertyPoint Pt(double t) => new(t, 2.0, 850.0, 0.002, 0.12);

    private static EnvironmentSpec Env(string id, string name) => new()
    {
        Id = id, Name = name, Kind = "liquid",
        Points = new[] { Pt(40), Pt(60), Pt(80), Pt(100), Pt(120) },
        TminC = 30, TmaxC = 130,
    };

    private static FlowSpec Flow(string id, bool hot, double tStart, double tTarget, params string[] apps) => new()
    {
        Id = id, Name = id, IsHot = hot, EnvironmentId = "E1",
        FlowRateKgH = 100_000, TStartC = tStart, TTargetC = tTarget,
        AllowedApparatusIds = apps, AllowSplit = true, MaxSplits = 2, DpBudgetMpa = 0.10,
    };

    private static ApparatusSpec App(string id, bool mandatory = false) => new()
    {
        Id = id, Name = id, IsExisting = true, IsMandatory = mandatory, Type = "shell&tube",
        AreaM2 = 120, TminC = 30, TmaxC = 130, K0Wm2K = 250,
        NominalVhMs = 1.0, NominalVcMs = 1.2, DpNomHMPa = 0.02, DpNomCMPa = 0.02,
    };

    public static ProjectSpec SmallProject() => new()
    {
        ProjectName = "Мини-полигон", InstallName = "АТ-5",
        Environments = new[] { Env("E1", "Нефть") },
        Flows = new[] { Flow("F1", true, 240, 120, "A1", "A2"), Flow("F2", false, 40, 180, "A1", "A2") },
        Apparatus = new[] { App("A1", mandatory: true), App("A2"), App("A3") },
        QglobMw = 8.0,
    };

    [Fact]
    public void Project_Builds_And_Model_Consistent()
    {
        var p = SmallProject();
        Assert.Equal(2, p.Flows.Count);
        Assert.Equal(3, p.Apparatus.Count);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void State_IsImmutable_And_BuilderBuildsNew()
    {
        var baseState = new State
        {
            Committed = new[] { new AppInstance("A1", "F1", Side.Hot, 0, 1.0, false, "P0") },
            ResidualHot = new[] { new ResidualSegment("F1", true, 100_000, 240, 120, true, "P0") },
            ResidualCold = new[] { new ResidualSegment("F2", false, 100_000, 40, 180, true, "P0") },
            FreeApparatusIds = new[] { "A2", "A3" },
            QFixMw = 2.5,
        };

        var next = baseState.With(b =>
        {
            b.Commit(new AppInstance("A2", "F2", Side.Cold, 1, 1.0, false, "P0"));
            b.RemoveFree("A2");
            b.QFixMw += 1.0;
            b.Depth = baseState.Depth + 1;
            b.ParentKey = baseState.CanonicalKey;
        });

        Assert.Equal(2, next.Committed.Count);
        Assert.Single(next.FreeApparatusIds);
        Assert.Equal(3.5, next.QFixMw, 9);
        // базовое состояние не изменилось
        Assert.Single(baseState.Committed);
        Assert.Equal(2, baseState.FreeApparatusIds.Count);
        Assert.Equal(2.5, baseState.QFixMw, 9);
        Assert.Equal(0, baseState.Depth);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CutParameters_Defaults_Match_AppendixA()
    {
        var cp = new CutParameters();
        Assert.Equal(20.0, cp.DtolOverlapC, 9);
        Assert.Equal(10.0, cp.TminC, 9);
        Assert.Equal(200, cp.BeamW);
        Assert.Null(cp.DMaxOverride);
        Assert.Equal(2, cp.NSplitMax);
        Assert.Equal(0.10, cp.FMinFrac, 9);
        Assert.Equal(0.5, cp.KMin, 9);
        Assert.Equal(2.0, cp.KMax, 9);
        Assert.Equal(2300.0, cp.ReMin, 6);
        Assert.Equal(500_000, cp.OpenLimit);
        Assert.Equal(100, cp.NIterMax);
        Assert.Equal(0.001, cp.EpsConv, 12);
        Assert.Equal(10.0, cp.DGridC, 9);
        Assert.Equal(0.01, cp.DpTolFrac, 9);
        Assert.Equal(0.001, cp.EpsUbRel, 9);
        Assert.Equal(0.02, cp.EpsMarginRel, 9);
        Assert.Equal(26.0, cp.WorkShiftC, 9);
        Assert.Equal(2.0, cp.GMaxWorkMult, 9);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 10, 20, 30, 40, 50 }, cp.SplitGridPct);
    }
}

public class SoftVectorTests
{
    private static SoftViolation V(string rule, SoftSeverity sev, string msg = "") => new(rule, sev, msg);

    [Fact]
    public void Sorted_Descending_By_Severity()
    {
        var sv = new SoftVector(V("R1", SoftSeverity.Low), V("R2", SoftSeverity.High), V("R3", SoftSeverity.Med));
        Assert.Equal(SoftSeverity.High, sv[0].Severity);
        Assert.Equal(SoftSeverity.Med, sv[1].Severity);
        Assert.Equal(SoftSeverity.Low, sv[2].Severity);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void LowerSeverity_Is_Better()
    {
        var a = new SoftVector(V("R1", SoftSeverity.Low));
        var b = new SoftVector(V("R1", SoftSeverity.Med));
        Assert.True(a.NotWorseThan(b));
        Assert.False(b.NotWorseThan(a));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Lexicographic_By_Severity_Vector()
    {
        // [High] хуже, чем [Med, Med] (лексикографика по убыванию severity)
        var a = new SoftVector(V("R1", SoftSeverity.Med), V("R2", SoftSeverity.Med));
        var b = new SoftVector(V("R1", SoftSeverity.High));
        Assert.True(a.NotWorseThan(b));
        // [Med, Low] лучше [Med, Med] при равном префиксе
        var c = new SoftVector(V("R1", SoftSeverity.Med), V("R2", SoftSeverity.Low));
        var d = new SoftVector(V("R1", SoftSeverity.Med), V("R2", SoftSeverity.Med));
        Assert.True(c.NotWorseThan(d));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Equal_Severity_Pattern_Compared_By_Count()
    {
        var a = new SoftVector(V("R1", SoftSeverity.Med));
        var b = new SoftVector(V("R1", SoftSeverity.Med), V("R2", SoftSeverity.Med));
        Assert.True(a.NotWorseThan(b));
        Assert.False(b.NotWorseThan(a));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Add_Keeps_Sorting_And_Empty_Is_Best()
    {
        Assert.True(SoftVector.Empty.NotWorseThan(new SoftVector(V("R1", SoftSeverity.Low))));
        var sv = SoftVector.Empty.Add(V("R1", SoftSeverity.Crit));
        Assert.Single(sv);
        Assert.Equal(SoftSeverity.Crit, sv[0].Severity);
    }
}








=== FILE: pinchess/tests/Pinchess.Tests/E2Tests.cs ===








using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Search;
using Pinchess.Engine.Sim;
using Xunit;

namespace Pinchess.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// Э2 (патч): строгий Г1 с опцией StrictSafeUb — перестановочно-безопасный UB; честный сертификат.
/// История: StrictBnB на факт-сцене завершал «полный» обход с Q_best=23,084 против beam 27,739
/// (потеря 4,66 МВт) при UB&lt;Q_best и GuaranteeComplete=True — Г1 резал по заниженной хвостовой UB,
/// исчерпание MaxStates не помечалось, а rescue при Q_best=-inf зацикливался (rank/rescue без pop).
/// </summary>
public class E2Tests
{
    [Fact]
    public void UbGlobal_CoversLightSceneBest()
    {
        var p = At5Fixtures.Scene();
        var ubGlobal = UpperBound.UbGlobalMw(p);
        Assert.True(ubGlobal >= 8.185 + 1e-6,
            $"глобальная оболочка лайта {ubGlobal:0.000} МВт < Q_best=8,185");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UbGlobal_CoversFactSceneBeams()
    {
        var p = At5Fixtures.FactScene();
        var ubGlobal = UpperBound.UbGlobalMw(p);
        Assert.True(ubGlobal >= 27.739 - 1e-6,
            $"глобальная оболочка факт-сцены {ubGlobal:0.000} МВт < beam-оптимума 27,739");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StrictSafeUb_MaxOfUpperBounds_CoversRootAndR1Child()
    {
        var p = At5Fixtures.FactScene();
        p.Parameters = p.Parameters with { StrictSafeUb = true };
        var baseState = At5Fixtures.FactBaseState(p);
        var kept = baseState.Committed.Where(i => i.ApparatusId is not ("Т-3" or "Т-4")).ToArray();
        var subset = new State { Committed = kept, Splits = baseState.Splits, FreeApparatusIds = new[] { "Т-3", "Т-4" } };
        var sim = new Simulator(p);
        var simP = sim.Simulate(subset, SimLevel.Coarse);
        var tails = UpperBound.ResidualTails(p, subset, simP);
        double ubTail = UpperBound.UbTailMw(p, subset, simP, tails, 0, coarse: true);
        double ubApp = UpperBound.UbApparatusMw(p, subset, coarse: true);
        double ubGlob = UpperBound.UbGlobalMw(p);
        double ubSafe = Math.Max(Math.Max(ubTail, ubApp), ubGlob);
        Assert.True(ubSafe >= simP.QTotalMw - 1e-6,
            $"UB-safe={ubSafe:0.000} < Q(корня)={simP.QTotalMw:0.000}");
        var child = StateOps.ApplyMove(p, subset, new Move(MoveKind.R1Swap, "Т-12", HotFlowId: "MZ", HotPosition: 2, ColdFlowId: "C1", ColdPosition: 8));
        var simC = sim.Simulate(child, SimLevel.Coarse);
        Assert.True(ubSafe >= simC.QTotalMw - 1e-6,
            $"Э2: UB-safe(родителя)={ubSafe:0.000} < Q(R1-ребёнка)={simC.QTotalMw:0.000}");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StrictCertificate_HonestOnStateLimit()
    {
        var p = At5Fixtures.Scene();
        var st = At5Fixtures.BaseState(p);
        var run = new SearchRun(SearchModeKind.StrictBnB, 200, 50, st, null, CancellationToken.None);
        var o = new SearchEngine(p, run).Run();
        var c = o.Certificate;
        Assert.False(c.GuaranteeComplete, "при исчерпании MaxStates гарантия не может быть полной");
        Assert.Contains("MaxStates", c.DegradedReason ?? "");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StrictCertificate_HonestOnFactSubset()
    {
        // строгий (дефолт, эвристический UB) на факт-подмножестве: потеря оптимума ДОЛЖНА быть
        // подписана сертификатом (гарантия снята с причиной Э2/UB/MaxStates), а не молчаливой
        var p = At5Fixtures.FactScene();
        var baseState = At5Fixtures.FactBaseState(p);
        var kept = baseState.Committed.Where(i => i.ApparatusId is not ("Т-3" or "Т-4")).ToArray();
        var subset = new State { Committed = kept, Splits = baseState.Splits, FreeApparatusIds = new[] { "Т-3", "Т-4" } };
        var run = new SearchRun(SearchModeKind.StrictBnB, 200, 20_000, subset, null, CancellationToken.None);
        var o = new SearchEngine(p, run).Run();
        var c = o.Certificate;
        Assert.False(c.GuaranteeComplete,
            $"сертификат строгого режима с эвристической UB не может подписывать «полную» гарантию (Q={c.QBestMw:0.000}, UB={c.UbBestMw:0.000})");
        Assert.False(string.IsNullOrEmpty(c.DegradedReason), "снятая гарантия должна иметь причину");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StrictSafeUb_FactSubset_NoOptimumLoss()
    {
        // StrictSafeUb=true + ранняя честная деградация (OpenLimit=100): поиск не теряет оптимум —
        // R2-спуск в beam-фазе ведёт к complete не хуже факт-базы
        var p = At5Fixtures.FactScene();
        p.Parameters = p.Parameters with { StrictSafeUb = true, OpenLimit = 100 };
        var baseState = At5Fixtures.FactBaseState(p);
        var kept = baseState.Committed.Where(i => i.ApparatusId is not ("Т-3" or "Т-4")).ToArray();
        var subset = new State { Committed = kept, Splits = baseState.Splits, FreeApparatusIds = new[] { "Т-3", "Т-4" } };
        var run = new SearchRun(SearchModeKind.StrictBnB, 200, 40_000, subset, null, CancellationToken.None);
        var o = new SearchEngine(p, run).Run();
        var c = o.Certificate;
        var sim = new Simulator(p);
        var factSubset = sim.Simulate(subset, SimLevel.Coarse);
        double epsLayer = Math.Max(p.Parameters.EpsUbRel * p.QglobMw, 0.01);
        Assert.True(c.QBestMw + epsLayer >= factSubset.QTotalMw,
            $"строгий (safe) потерял оптимум: Q_best={c.QBestMw:0.000} << факт={factSubset.QTotalMw:0.000} (причина={c.DegradedReason})");
    }
}








=== FILE: pinchess/tests/Pinchess.Tests/EnvelopeTests.cs ===







namespace Pinchess.Tests;

using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Search;
using Pinchess.Engine.Sim;
using Xunit;

/// <summary>
/// S8 шаг 1: вогнутая оболочка UB при делениях (протокол совещания 18:53, пункт А).
/// Свойство: любая конфигурация делений даёт поточечно Σ_ij min(α_i·h, β_j·c) ≤
/// min(M·h, N·c); интеграл оболочки — доказуемо верхняя оценка результата ребёнка.
/// Проверяется property-based перебором долей на случайных кривых (полный аналог
/// «UB(parent) ≥ Q(child)» на уровне пары сегментов — до подключения R3-симулятора).
/// </summary>
public class EnvelopeTests
{
    private static EnvironmentSpec Env(params (double T, double Cp)[] pts) => new()
    {
        Id = "E1", Name = "тест-среда", Kind = "liquid",
        Points = pts.Select(p => new PropertyPoint(p.T, p.Cp, 800.0, 1e-3, 0.15)).ToList(),
        TminC = pts[0].T, TmaxC = pts[^1].T,
    };

    private static ResidualTail Tail(bool hot, double rate, double tLow, double tHigh, double qResidualMw) =>
        new("F", hot, rate, Math.Min(tLow, tHigh), Math.Max(tLow, tHigh), qResidualMw);

    private static double QResidual(EnthalpyCurve c, double rate, double tLow, double tHigh) =>
        rate * (c.H(tHigh) - c.H(tLow)) / 3.6e6; // МВт (кДж/ч → МВт)

    /// <summary>Независимая трапеция (шаг 0,25 °C) — не та сетка, что в реализации.</summary>
    private static double Integrate(Func<double, double> g, double lo, double hi)
    {
        const double step = 0.25;
        double sum = 0;
        for (double x = lo; x < hi - 1e-12; x += step)
        {
            double x2 = Math.Min(x + step, hi);
            sum += 0.5 * (g(x) + g(x2)) * (x2 - x);
        }
        return sum;
    }

    private static readonly double[] FractionGrid = { 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.40, 0.50 };

    private static EnvironmentSpec RandomCurve(Random rnd)
    {
        var pts = new List<(double, double)>();
        for (int i = 0; i <= 12; i++)
            pts.Add((20.0 + i * 15.0, 1.0 + rnd.NextDouble() * 3.0));
        return Env(pts.ToArray());
    }

    /// <summary>Оболочка ≥ интеграл min(h,c) при любых M,N ≥ 1 (поточечная миноранта).</summary>
    [Fact]
    public void Envelope_DominatesNaiveOverlap()
    {
        var rnd = new Random(20261115);
        for (int k = 0; k < 20; k++)
        {
            var envH = RandomCurve(rnd); var envC = RandomCurve(rnd);
            var ch = new EnthalpyCurve(envH); var cc = new EnthalpyCurve(envC);
            var hot = Tail(true, 40000, 60, 170, QResidual(ch, 40000, 60, 170));
            var cold = Tail(false, 50000, 25, 160, QResidual(cc, 50000, 25, 160));
            foreach (var (m, n) in new[] { (1.0, 1.0), (2.0, 1.0), (1.0, 2.0), (3.0, 3.0) })
                Assert.True(
                    UpperBound.QPairOverlapMw(hot, cold, ch, cc, 6.0, hotBranchesMax: m, coldBranchesMax: n) + 1e-12
                    >= UpperBound.QPairOverlapMw(hot, cold, ch, cc, 6.0),
                    $"оболочка M={m} N={n} ниже наивной min(h,c)");
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// ГЛАВНОЕ property-based свойство (деление холодного на 2 ветви):
    /// UB(M=1, N=2) ≥ ∫min(h, α·c) + ∫min(h, (1−α)·c) для всех долей α сетки.
    /// Именно это свойство раньше нарушал наивный UB (min(h,c) не супераддитивно).
    /// </summary>
    [Fact]
    public void Envelope_SafeForColdSplitTwoBranches()
    {
        var rnd = new Random(7);
        for (int k = 0; k < 12; k++)
        {
            var envH = RandomCurve(rnd); var envC = RandomCurve(rnd);
            var ch = new EnthalpyCurve(envH); var cc = new EnthalpyCurve(envC);
            var hot = Tail(true, 40000, 60, 170, QResidual(ch, 40000, 60, 170));
            var cold = Tail(false, 50000, 25, 160, QResidual(cc, 50000, 25, 160));
            double lo = Math.Max(hot.TLowC, cold.TLowC + 6.0);
            double hi = Math.Min(hot.THighC, cold.THighC + 6.0);
            double ub = UpperBound.QPairOverlapMw(hot, cold, ch, cc, 6.0, hotBranchesMax: 1.0, coldBranchesMax: 2.0);
            foreach (var f in FractionGrid)
            {
                double qReal = Integrate(x => Math.Min(
                        40000 * (ch.H(x + 6.0 + 1.0) - ch.H(x + 6.0)) / 3600.0,
                        f * 50000 * (cc.H(x + 1.0) - cc.H(x)) / 3600.0), lo, hi)
                    + Integrate(x => Math.Min(
                        40000 * (ch.H(x + 6.0 + 1.0) - ch.H(x + 6.0)) / 3600.0,
                        (1 - f) * 50000 * (cc.H(x + 1.0) - cc.H(x)) / 3600.0), lo, hi);
                qReal /= 1e6;
                Assert.True(ub + 1e-9 >= qReal,
                    $"нарушение оболочки: UB={ub:0.000000} < Q_дел={qReal:0.000000} при f={f}");
            }
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Двойное деление (горячий и холодный по 2 ветви): UB(M=2, N=2) ≥ Σ_ij ∫min(α_i h, β_j c).</summary>
    [Fact]
    public void Envelope_SafeForDoubleSplit()
    {
        var rnd = new Random(99);
        for (int k = 0; k < 12; k++)
        {
            var envH = RandomCurve(rnd); var envC = RandomCurve(rnd);
            var ch = new EnthalpyCurve(envH); var cc = new EnthalpyCurve(envC);
            var hot = Tail(true, 40000, 60, 170, QResidual(ch, 40000, 60, 170));
            var cold = Tail(false, 50000, 25, 160, QResidual(cc, 50000, 25, 160));
            double lo = Math.Max(hot.TLowC, cold.TLowC + 6.0);
            double hi = Math.Min(hot.THighC, cold.THighC + 6.0);
            double ub = UpperBound.QPairOverlapMw(hot, cold, ch, cc, 6.0, hotBranchesMax: 2.0, coldBranchesMax: 2.0);
            foreach (var fa in FractionGrid)
            foreach (var fb in FractionGrid)
            {
                double qReal = 0;
                foreach (var (ah, bh) in new[] { (fa, 1 - fa), (1 - fa, fa) })
                foreach (var (ac, bc) in new[] { (fb, 1 - fb), (1 - fb, fb) })
                    qReal += Integrate(x => Math.Min(
                        ah * 40000 * (ch.H(x + 6.0 + 1.0) - ch.H(x + 6.0)) / 3600.0,
                        bc * 50000 * (cc.H(x + 1.0) - cc.H(x)) / 3600.0), lo, hi);
                qReal /= 1e6;
                Assert.True(ub + 1e-9 >= qReal,
                    $"нарушение оболочки (2×2): UB={ub:0.000000} < Q_дел={qReal:0.000000} при α={fa} β={fb}");
            }
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Аналитика на константных кривых: оболочка min(M·h, N·c) — точное окно.</summary>
    [Fact]
    public void Envelope_ConstantCpAnalytic()
    {
        var ch = new EnthalpyCurve(Env((10, 2.0), (90, 2.0)));
        var cc = new EnthalpyCurve(Env((10, 2.0), (90, 2.0)));
        var hot = Tail(true, 36000, 40, 80, 1e6);
        var cold = Tail(false, 36000, 0, 100, 1e6);
        // h = c = 36000·2/3600 = 20 кВт/К; окно ξ: [max(40, 0+10), min(80, 110)] = [40,80]
        double expected = 40 * 20.0 / 1000.0; // 0,8 МВт при M=N=1
        Assert.InRange(UpperBound.QPairOverlapMw(hot, cold, ch, cc, 10.0), expected - 1e-9, expected + 1e-9);
        // M=2, N=1: g = min(40, 20) = 20 → тот же интеграл 0,8 (min упирается в холодный)
        Assert.InRange(UpperBound.QPairOverlapMw(hot, cold, ch, cc, 10.0, hotBranchesMax: 2.0), expected - 1e-9, expected + 1e-9);
        // M=1, N=2: g = min(20, 40) = 20 → 0,8
        Assert.InRange(UpperBound.QPairOverlapMw(hot, cold, ch, cc, 10.0, coldBranchesMax: 2.0), expected - 1e-9, expected + 1e-9);
        // M=3, N=2: g = min(60, 40) = 40 → 1,6 МВт (оболочка разрешает деление холодного)
        Assert.InRange(UpperBound.QPairOverlapMw(hot, cold, ch, cc, 10.0, hotBranchesMax: 3.0, coldBranchesMax: 2.0),
            1.6 - 1e-9, 1.6 + 1e-9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void BranchesMax_RespectsAllowSplit()
    {
        var project = new ProjectSpec
        {
            Flows = new List<FlowSpec>
            {
                new() { Id = "C1", IsHot = false, AllowSplit = true, MaxSplits = 2 },
                new() { Id = "H1", IsHot = true, AllowSplit = false },
                new() { Id = "H2", IsHot = true, AllowSplit = true, MaxSplits = 1 },
            },
        };
        Assert.Equal(3.0, UpperBound.BranchesMaxOf(project, "C1"));
        Assert.Equal(1.0, UpperBound.BranchesMaxOf(project, "H1"));
        Assert.Equal(2.0, UpperBound.BranchesMaxOf(project, "H2"));
        Assert.Equal(1.0, UpperBound.BranchesMaxOf(project, "нет-такого"));
    }
}






=== FILE: pinchess/tests/Pinchess.Tests/FactSceneTests.cs ===








using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Search;
using Pinchess.Engine.Sim;
using Xunit;

namespace Pinchess.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// S8: факт-сцена (FactSceneBuilder) и непрерывная доля (Нелдер–Мид, шаг 7).
/// Факт-сцена — сабсет «Ответа Ильскому 3»: допущения перечислены в At5Fixtures.FactScene.
/// </summary>
public class FactSceneTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void FactScene_BalancesWithinTolerance()
    {
        var p = At5Fixtures.FactScene();
        var st = At5Fixtures.FactBaseState(p);
        var sim = new Simulator(p);
        var res = sim.Simulate(st, SimLevel.Fine);
        // ФАКТ-АНОМАЛИИ (письмо, раздел 4): балансы факт-цепочки по нефти и горячим сторонам НЕ БЬЮТСЯ
        // (Т-12: нефть получает 4,48 МВт против 1,96 от горячей стороны; Т-13: 3,42 против 1,79),
        // и в противоточном окне у части аппаратов горячая сторона оказывается холоднее холодной.
        // Поэтому требование «модель воспроизводит факт-нагрузки» НЕ ставится: модель находит СВОЙ
        // стационарный режим той же сети; несходимость и перекрестия факт-базы — ожидаемый признак аномалий.
        // Проверяем: механизм даёт осмысленный ответ (Q в физическом окне, без NaN и отрицательных нагрузок).
        Assert.True(res.QTotalMw > 8.0 && res.QTotalMw < 26.0,
            $"факт-сцена: осмысленный режим Q={res.QTotalMw:0.000} МВт (conv={res.AllConverged}; аномалии факта — несходящиеся балансы Т-12/Т-13/Т-8)");
        foreach (var a in res.Apparatus)
        {
            Assert.True(!double.IsNaN(a.ThOutC) && !double.IsNaN(a.TcOutC), $"{a.ApparatusId}: NaN-температуры");
            Assert.True(a.QMw >= -1e-9, $"{a.ApparatusId}: отрицательная нагрузка {a.QMw:0.000}");
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void FactScene_SearchBeatsFactOnSubset()
    {
        // S8-бис: факт-сцена v3 (калиброванные площади, SleepTolC=20) — скан от ПОДМНОЖЕСТВА
        // факт-базы (Т-3/Т-4 свободны): оптимум не может быть хуже базы на том же подмножестве.
        var p = At5Fixtures.FactScene();
        var baseState = At5Fixtures.FactBaseState(p);
        var kept = baseState.Committed.Where(i => i.ApparatusId is not ("Т-3" or "Т-4")).ToArray();
        var subset = new State { Committed = kept, Splits = baseState.Splits, FreeApparatusIds = new[] { "Т-3", "Т-4" } };
        var sim = new Simulator(p);
        var factSubset = sim.Simulate(subset, SimLevel.Coarse);
        var run = new SearchRun(SearchModeKind.BeamSearch, 200, 80_000, subset, null, CancellationToken.None);
        var o = new SearchEngine(p, run).Run();
        var c = o.Certificate;
        Assert.False(double.IsNegativeInfinity(c.QBestMw), "скан должен найти хотя бы факт-раскладку подмножества");
        Assert.True(c.QBestMw + 1e-9 >= factSubset.QTotalMw,
            $"оптимум не может быть хуже базы: {c.QBestMw:0.000} < {factSubset.QTotalMw:0.000}");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SplitRefiner_NM_ImprovesAndIsDeterministic()
    {
        var project = At5Fixtures.Scene();
        // разрешаем деление холодного потока для этого теста
        project.Flows = project.Flows.Select(f => f.Id == "C1" ? f with { AllowSplit = true, MaxSplits = 1 } : f).ToList();
        var st0 = StateOps.InitialState(project);
        var st1 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st0,
            new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "Т-9", HotFlowId: "G4", HotPosition: 0, ColdFlowId: "C2", ColdPosition: 0));
        // деление холодного потока C1 (цепочка пуста — только pre-сегмент): сетка 0,5 — старт
        var st2 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st1,
            new Move(MoveKind.R3Split, "", SegmentId: "C1:pre", SplitFractionA: 0.5));
        var st3 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st2,
            new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "Т-7", HotFlowId: "G4", HotPosition: 1,
                ColdFlowId: "C1", ColdPosition: 0, SegmentId: "C1:pre|A"));

        var sim = new Simulator(project);
        var resGrid = sim.Simulate(st3, SimLevel.Fine);
        double qGrid = resGrid.AllConverged ? resGrid.QTotalMw : double.NegativeInfinity;

        var (refined, qRef) = SplitRefiner.Refine(project, st3, sim);
        Assert.True(qRef >= qGrid - 1e-9, $"NM не должен ухудшать: сетка {qGrid:0.000} против NM {qRef:0.000}");

        var (refined2, qRef2) = SplitRefiner.Refine(project, st3, sim);
        Assert.Equal(qRef, qRef2, 9); // детерминизм: два прогона дают одно значение
        Assert.Equal(refined.Splits[0].FractionA, refined2.Splits[0].FractionA, 9);
    }
}








=== FILE: pinchess/tests/Pinchess.Tests/GridDiagramTests.cs ===







using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Search;
using Pinchess.Engine.Validation;
using Pinchess.Engine.Sim;
using Xunit;

namespace Pinchess.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// UI2a: сетчатая диаграмма — конвертация «линии + кружки» в проект (потоки/аппараты/сеть/позиции).
/// Закрепляет правило: порядок кружков вдоль линии = PositionIndex; сеть — boundary на концах,
/// apparatus-узел на кружке; симуляция по AppInstance.
/// </summary>
public class GridDiagramTests
{
    private static (ProjectSpec Project, List<AppInstance> Instances) BuildSmoke()
    {
        var flows = new FlowSpec[]
        {
            new() { Id = "F1", Name = "Нефть", IsHot = true, EnvironmentId = "CRD",
                FlowRateKgH = 100_000, TStartC = 200, TTargetC = 40,
                AllowedApparatusIds = new[] { "N-01" }, DpBudgetMpa = 0.1 },
            new() { Id = "F2", Name = "Вода", IsHot = false, EnvironmentId = "WTR",
                FlowRateKgH = 50_000, TStartC = 20, TTargetC = 80,
                AllowedApparatusIds = new[] { "N-01" }, DpBudgetMpa = 0.1 },
        };
        var apparatus = new ApparatusSpec[]
        {
            new() { Id = "N-01", Name = "Новый аппарат", AreaM2 = 100, K0Wm2K = 250, TminC = 0, TmaxC = 700 },
        };
        var project = new ProjectSpec
        {
            ProjectName = "Смоук сетчатой диаграммы",
            Environments = Pinchess.Engine.Props.DefaultEnvironments.Create(),
            Flows = flows,
            Apparatus = apparatus
        };
        var instances = new List<AppInstance>
        {
            new("N-01", "F1", Side.Hot, 0, 1.0, false, "P0"),
            new("N-01", "F2", Side.Cold, 0, 1.0, false, "P0"),
        };
        return (project, instances);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GridDiagram_ProjectValidates()
    {
        var (project, _) = BuildSmoke();
        var report = ProjectValidator.Validate(project);
        Assert.Equal(0, report.Errors);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GridDiagram_SimulatesByPositions()
    {
        var (project, instances) = BuildSmoke();
        var sim = new Simulator(project);
        var result = sim.Simulate(instances, SimLevel.Coarse);
        Assert.True(result.QTotalMw > 0.5, $"Q={result.QTotalMw:0.000}");
        Assert.True(result.AllConverged, "схема из диаграммы должна сходиться");
        var r = result.Apparatus.Single(x => x.ApparatusId == "N-01");
        Assert.True(r.ThInC > r.ThOutC, "горячая сторона: Th на входе выше, чем на выходе");
        Assert.True(r.TcOutC > r.TcInC, "холодная сторона: Tc на выходе выше, чем на входе");
    }
}








=== FILE: pinchess/tests/Pinchess.Tests/GridProjectMapperTests.cs ===








using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Grid;
using Pinchess.Engine.Io;
using Pinchess.Engine.Search;
using Pinchess.Engine.Sim;
using Pinchess.Engine.Validation;
using Xunit;

namespace Pinchess.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// Г3 (решение ревьюера КП2.1, 15:19 18.11): чистая логика конвертации сетчатой диаграммы
/// в проект — GridProjectMapper — без WinForms. Плюс полный путь «с нуля» (запрос владельца
/// 16:26 18.11): пустой проект → среды → сетка → проект → сохранение/загрузка → валидация →
/// beam-скан → Best. Сцена — теоретический проект «Т-Р1».
/// </summary>
public class GridProjectMapperTests
{
    private static List<GridLine> Lines() => new()
    {
        new GridLine { Id = "F1", Name = "Горячий", IsHot = true, EnvId = "CRD",
            RateKgH = 100_000, TStart = 200, TTarget = 120, DpBudget = 0.1,
            Points = new List<GridPoint> { new(40, 120), new(560, 120) } },
        new GridLine { Id = "F2", Name = "Холодный", IsHot = false, EnvId = "WTR",
            RateKgH = 30_000, TStart = 40, TTarget = 110, DpBudget = 0.1,
            Points = new List<GridPoint> { new(40, 320), new(560, 320) } },
    };

    private static List<GridApp> Apps() => new()
    {
        new GridApp { Id = "A1", Name = "А-1", AreaM2 = 120, K0 = 250, HotLineId = "F1", HotSeg = 0, HotT = 0.2, ColdLineId = "F2", ColdSeg = 0, ColdT = 0.8 },
        new GridApp { Id = "A2", Name = "А-2", AreaM2 = 80, K0 = 200, HotLineId = "F1", HotSeg = 0, HotT = 0.5, ColdLineId = "F2", ColdSeg = 0, ColdT = 0.5 },
        new GridApp { Id = "A3", Name = "А-3", AreaM2 = 50, K0 = 300, HotLineId = "F1", HotSeg = 0, HotT = 0.8, ColdLineId = "F2", ColdSeg = 0, ColdT = 0.2 },
    };

    private static ProjectSpec EmptyProject() => new() { ProjectName = "Т-Р1", Environments = Pinchess.Engine.Props.DefaultEnvironments.Create() };

    [Fact]
    public void Mapper_BuildProject_TransfersFields()
    {
        var spec = GridProjectMapper.BuildProject(EmptyProject(), Lines(), Apps());
        Assert.Equal(2, spec.Flows.Count);
        Assert.Equal(3, spec.Apparatus.Count);
        var f1 = spec.Flows[0];
        Assert.True(f1.IsHot);
        Assert.Equal(200, f1.TStartC);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "A1", "A2", "A3" }, f1.AllowedApparatusIds);
        Assert.Equal(250, spec.Apparatus[0].K0Wm2K);
        Assert.Equal(120, spec.Apparatus[0].AreaM2);
        Assert.Equal(1.0, spec.Apparatus[0].NominalVhMs); // v13: номинальные скорости — иначе П4 режет v=0
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Mapper_BuildInstances_PositionsAlongLine()
    {
        var spec = GridProjectMapper.BuildProject(EmptyProject(), Lines(), Apps());
        var inst = GridProjectMapper.BuildInstances(spec, Lines(), Apps());
        // горячая линия: порядок по T: A1(0.2) → A2(0.5) → A3(0.8)
        var hot = inst.Where(i => i.Side == Side.Hot).OrderBy(i => i.PositionIndex).ToList();
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "A1", "A2", "A3" }, hot.Select(i => i.ApparatusId));
        Assert.All(hot, i => Assert.Equal(hot.IndexOf(i), i.PositionIndex));
        // холодная линия — порядок по T: A3(0.2) → A2(0.5) → A1(0.8)
        var cold = inst.Where(i => i.Side == Side.Cold).OrderBy(i => i.PositionIndex).ToList();
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "A3", "A2", "A1" }, cold.Select(i => i.ApparatusId));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Mapper_Project_RoundTrip_KeepsInstances()
    {
        var spec = GridProjectMapper.BuildProject(EmptyProject(), Lines(), Apps());
        spec.InitialInstances = GridProjectMapper.BuildInstances(spec, Lines(), Apps());
        string path = System.IO.Path.Combine(System.IO.Path.GetTempPath(),
            $"pinchess_g3_{System.Guid.NewGuid():N}.json");
        try
        {
            ProjectJsonIO.Save(spec, path);
            var (loaded, report) = ProjectJsonIO.Load(path);
            Assert.NotNull(loaded);
            Assert.Equal(0, report.Errors);
            Assert.Equal(6, loaded!.InitialInstances.Count);
            Assert.Equal(2, loaded.Flows.Count);
        }
        finally { System.IO.File.Delete(path); }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EmptyProject_FailsValidation_AndMapperFixes()
    {
        // «с нуля»: пустой проект (как NewProject) сам по себе валидатор НЕ отсекает
        // (нет обязательных потоков) — пустота отсеивается в UI до скана
        // (RunScanCurrentProject: «Проект пуст…»); после отрисовки сетки и mapper — ошибок нет.
        var empty = EmptyProject();
        Assert.False(ProjectValidator.Validate(empty).HasErrors);

        var spec = GridProjectMapper.BuildProject(empty, Lines(), Apps());
        spec.InitialInstances = GridProjectMapper.BuildInstances(spec, Lines(), Apps());
        var report = ProjectValidator.Validate(spec);
        Assert.Equal(0, report.Errors);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EndToEnd_FromBlank_ToScanBeatsBaseline()
    {
        // Полный путь владельца: пустой проект → сетка → проект → сохранение → скан → Best.
        // Сценарий с заведомо неудачной расстановкой (прямоток): скан ОБЯЗАН найти прирост.
        // Скан — с пустого старта (StateOps.BlankState): beam не расширяет complete-узлы,
        // а InitialState с InitialInstances — это complete-факт-схема (Г1).
        var lines = Lines();
        foreach (var a in Apps()) { a.ColdT = a.HotT; } // прямоток: одинаковый порядок
        var spec = GridProjectMapper.BuildProject(EmptyProject(), lines, Apps());
        spec.QglobMw = 12.0;
        spec.InitialInstances = GridProjectMapper.BuildInstances(spec, lines, Apps());
        Assert.Equal(0, ProjectValidator.Validate(spec).Errors);

        var sim = new Simulator(spec);
        var baseRes = sim.Simulate(StateOps.InitialState(spec), SimLevel.Coarse);
        Assert.True(baseRes.QTotalMw > 0.1, $"База: {baseRes.QTotalMw:0.000}");

        var run = new SearchRun(SearchModeKind.BeamSearch, BeamW: 200, MaxStates: 20_000,
            WarmStart: StateOps.BlankState(spec), Seed: null, CancellationToken.None);
        var outcome = new SearchEngine(spec, run).Run();
        Assert.True(outcome.Best is not null, "Скан должен найти решение: " + outcome.Report?.ToString());
        var fine = sim.Simulate(outcome.Best!, SimLevel.Fine);
        Assert.True(fine.AllConverged);
        Assert.True(fine.QTotalMw > 0.1);
        // beam — эвристика: Q_best может слегка отставать от факт-базы (coarse/fine);
        // допуск 0.02 МВт — «не сильно хуже», полная гарантия только у StrictBnB
        Assert.True(outcome.Certificate.QBestMw >= baseRes.QTotalMw - 0.02,
            $"Q_best ({outcome.Certificate.QBestMw:0.000}) не сильно хуже базы ({baseRes.QTotalMw:0.000})");
    }
}








=== FILE: pinchess/tests/Pinchess.Tests/IoAndLogTests.cs ===







using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Io;
using Pinchess.Engine.Log;
using Xunit;

namespace Pinchess.Tests;

public class ProjectJsonIOTests : IDisposable
{
    private readonly string _dir;

    public ProjectJsonIOTests()
    {
        _dir = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "pinch_shahmaty_tests", Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
        Directory.CreateDirectory(_dir);
    }

    public void Dispose() => TryDelete(_dir);

    private static void TryDelete(string dir)
    {
        try { if (Directory.Exists(dir)) Directory.Delete(dir, recursive: true); }
        catch { /* в тестах не падать на блокировках */ }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RoundTrip_PreservesKeyFields()
    {
        var p = DomainTests.SmallProject() with { SchemaVersion = ProjectJsonIO.CurrentSchemaVersion };
        var path = Path.Combine(_dir, "project.json");
        ProjectJsonIO.Save(p, path);

        Assert.True(File.Exists(path));
        var (loaded, report) = ProjectJsonIO.Load(path);

        Assert.False(report.HasErrors);
        Assert.NotNull(loaded);
        Assert.Equal(p.ProjectName, loaded!.ProjectName);
        Assert.Equal(p.SchemaVersion, loaded.SchemaVersion);
        Assert.Equal(p.Flows.Count, loaded.Flows.Count);
        Assert.Equal(p.Apparatus.Count, loaded.Apparatus.Count);
        Assert.Equal(p.Parameters.DtolOverlapC, loaded.Parameters.DtolOverlapC, 9);
        Assert.Equal(p.Parameters.TminC, loaded.Parameters.TminC, 9);
        Assert.Equal(p.Environments[0].Points[0].CpKjKgK, loaded.Environments[0].Points[0].CpKjKgK, 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SchemaVersion_IsInFile()
    {
        var path = Path.Combine(_dir, "project.json");
        ProjectJsonIO.Save(DomainTests.SmallProject(), path);
        var json = File.ReadAllText(path);
        Assert.Contains($"\"schemaVersion\": {ProjectJsonIO.CurrentSchemaVersion}", json);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SchemaMismatch_IsReported()
    {
        var path = Path.Combine(_dir, "project.json");
        ProjectJsonIO.Save(DomainTests.SmallProject(), path);
        var json = File.ReadAllText(path)
            .Replace($"\"schemaVersion\": {ProjectJsonIO.CurrentSchemaVersion}", "\"schemaVersion\": 99");
        File.WriteAllText(path, json);

        var (_, report) = ProjectJsonIO.Load(path);
        Assert.Contains(report.Issues, i => i.Code == "SCHEMA_MISMATCH");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void InvalidJson_IsReported()
    {
        var path = Path.Combine(_dir, "broken.json");
        File.WriteAllText(path, "{ не-json");
        var (_, report) = ProjectJsonIO.Load(path);
        Assert.Contains(report.Issues, i => i.Code == "JSON_PARSE");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void MissingFile_IsReported()
    {
        var (_, report) = ProjectJsonIO.Load(Path.Combine(_dir, "nope.json"));
        Assert.Contains(report.Issues, i => i.Code == "FILE_NOT_FOUND");
    }
}

public class LogMissTests : IDisposable
{
    private readonly string _dir;

    public LogMissTests()
    {
        _dir = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "pinch_shahmaty_tests", "log_" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
        Directory.CreateDirectory(_dir);
        LogMiss.ResetForTest();
        LogMiss.LogDir = _dir;
        LogMiss.RotateBytes = 5_000_000;
    }

    public void Dispose()
    {
        LogMiss.ResetForTest();
        try { if (Directory.Exists(_dir)) Directory.Delete(_dir, recursive: true); }
        catch { }
    }

    private static string LogFile(string dir) => Path.Combine(dir, "LOG_miss.txt");

    [Fact]
    public void SessionHeader_WrittenOncePerSession()
    {
        LogMiss.Info("SYS", "первая строка");
        LogMiss.Info("SYS", "вторая строка");
        var text = File.ReadAllText(LogFile(_dir));
        Assert.Equal(1, CountOccurrences(text, "=== СЕССИЯ"));
        Assert.Contains("первая строка", text);
        Assert.Contains("вторая строка", text);
        Assert.Contains("[INFO] [SYS]", text);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NewSession_AfterReset()
    {
        LogMiss.Info("SYS", "сессия 1");
        LogMiss.ResetForTest();
        LogMiss.Info("SYS", "сессия 2");
        var text = File.ReadAllText(LogFile(_dir));
        Assert.Equal(2, CountOccurrences(text, "=== СЕССИЯ"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Rotation_ProducesPrevFile()
    {
        LogMiss.RotateBytes = 1; // ротация при каждом превышении
        LogMiss.Info("SYS", "строка, создающая файл");
        LogMiss.Info("SYS", "строка, триггерящая ротацию");
        Assert.True(File.Exists(Path.Combine(_dir, "LOG_miss_prev.txt")));
        Assert.True(File.Exists(LogFile(_dir)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AllLevels_AreWritten()
    {
        LogMiss.Info("SYS", "i");
        LogMiss.Evt("UI", "e");
        LogMiss.Warn("CUT", "w");
        LogMiss.Err("SIM", "x");
        var text = File.ReadAllText(LogFile(_dir));
        Assert.Contains("[INFO]", text);
        Assert.Contains("[EVT]", text);
        Assert.Contains("[WARN]", text);
        Assert.Contains("[ERR]", text);
    }

    private static int CountOccurrences(string text, string token)
    {
        int count = 0, idx = 0;
        while ((idx = text.IndexOf(token, idx, StringComparison.Ordinal)) >= 0)
        {
            count++;
            idx += token.Length;
        }
        return count;
    }
}








=== FILE: pinchess/tests/Pinchess.Tests/NoSolutionTests.cs ===









using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Search;
using Xunit;

namespace Pinchess.Tests;

/// <summary>S9: сценарий «НЕТ ДОПУСТИМОГО РЕШЕНИЯ» — движок не возвращает пустой ответ молча.</summary>
public class NoSolutionTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void NoSolution_IncompatibleTemperatures_ProducesReport()
    {
        // горячая сторона холоднее холодной при любом сопряжении → все вставки отсекаются (П1)
        var p = new ProjectSpec
        {
            ProjectName = "Несовместимая сцена",
            Environments = Pinchess.Engine.Props.DefaultEnvironments.Create(),
            Flows = new FlowSpec[]
            {
                new() { Id = "F1", Name = "Холодная горячая сторона", IsHot = true, EnvironmentId = "WTR",
                    FlowRateKgH = 50_000, TStartC = 60, TTargetC = 40, AllowedApparatusIds = new[] { "A1" }, DpBudgetMpa = 0.2 },
                new() { Id = "F2", Name = "Горячая холодная сторона", IsHot = false, EnvironmentId = "WTR",
                    FlowRateKgH = 50_000, TStartC = 120, TTargetC = 160, AllowedApparatusIds = new[] { "A1" }, DpBudgetMpa = 0.2 },
            },
            Apparatus = new ApparatusSpec[]
            {
                new() { Id = "A1", Name = "А-1", IsMandatory = true, AreaM2 = 100, K0Wm2K = 250, TminC = 0, TmaxC = 400, NominalVhMs = 1.0, NominalVcMs = 1.0 },
            },
            QglobMw = 5.0,
        };
        var st = StateOps.InitialState(p);
        var run = new SearchRun(SearchModeKind.BeamSearch, 50, 20_000, st, null, CancellationToken.None);
        var o = new SearchEngine(p, run).Run();
        Assert.True(o.Best is null, "несовместимая сцена не должна иметь complete-решения");
        Assert.NotNull(o.Report);
        var r = o.Report!;
        Assert.False(string.IsNullOrEmpty(r.Summary));
        Assert.True(r.TopCuts.Count > 0, "отчёт должен назвать отсекающие правила");
        Assert.Contains(r.MandatoryUnplaced, m => m.Contains("A1"));
        Assert.Contains("Tmin", string.Join(" ", r.Notes));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NoSolution_SmartScene_DoesNotProduceReport()
    {
        // нормальная сцена S3-смоука: решение есть — отчёта нет
        var p = new ProjectSpec
        {
            ProjectName = "Смоук S7",
            Environments = Pinchess.Engine.Props.DefaultEnvironments.Create(),
            Flows = new FlowSpec[]
            {
                new() { Id = "F1", Name = "Нефть", IsHot = true, EnvironmentId = "CRD",
                    FlowRateKgH = 100_000, TStartC = 200, TTargetC = 120, AllowedApparatusIds = new[] { "A1", "A2", "A3" }, DpBudgetMpa = 0.1 },
                new() { Id = "F2", Name = "Вода", IsHot = false, EnvironmentId = "WTR",
                    FlowRateKgH = 50_000, TStartC = 40, TTargetC = 180, AllowedApparatusIds = new[] { "A1", "A2", "A3" }, DpBudgetMpa = 0.1 },
            },
            Apparatus = new ApparatusSpec[]
            {
                new() { Id = "A1", Name = "А-1", AreaM2 = 120, K0Wm2K = 250, TminC = 0, TmaxC = 400, NominalVhMs = 1.0, NominalVcMs = 1.0 },
                new() { Id = "A2", Name = "А-2", AreaM2 = 80, K0Wm2K = 200, TminC = 0, TmaxC = 400, NominalVhMs = 1.0, NominalVcMs = 1.0 },
                new() { Id = "A3", Name = "А-3", AreaM2 = 50, K0Wm2K = 300, TminC = 0, TmaxC = 400, NominalVhMs = 1.0, NominalVcMs = 1.0 },
            },
            QglobMw = 12.0,
        };
        var st = StateOps.InitialState(p);
        var run = new SearchRun(SearchModeKind.BeamSearch, 50, 20_000, st, null, CancellationToken.None);
        var o = new SearchEngine(p, run).Run();
        Assert.True(o.Best is not null, "нормальная сцена имеет решение");
        Assert.Null(o.Report);
    }
}








=== FILE: pinchess/tests/Pinchess.Tests/PhysicsTests.cs ===








using Pinchess.Engine.Calib;
using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Physics;
using Pinchess.Engine.Sim;
using Xunit;

namespace Pinchess.Tests;

public class KCorrelationsTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void DittusBoelter_Scales_As_Re08()
    {
        double nu1 = KCorrelations.NusseltDittusBoelter(12000, 5);
        double nu2 = KCorrelations.NusseltDittusBoelter(24000, 5);
        double ratio = nu2 / nu1;
        Assert.Equal(Math.Pow(2, 0.8), ratio, 4);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Laminar_Falls_Sharp()
    {
        // мазут: Re 1500 (ламинарный, ST) против Re 12000 (турбулентный, DB) при Pr = 50
        double nuLam = KCorrelations.NusseltSiederTate(1500, 50, 0.02);
        double nuTurb = KCorrelations.NusseltDittusBoelter(12000, 50);
        double ratio = nuLam / nuTurb;
        Assert.True(ratio < 0.15, $"Отношение h_лам/h_турб = {ratio:0.00} — падение должно быть резким (K падает в разы)");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Nusselt_Monotonic_Across_Regimes()
    {
        // нет провала на стыках 2300/10000
        double prev = KCorrelations.Nusselt(500, 5, 0.02);
        for (double re = 1000; re <= 13000; re += 250)
        {
            double nu = KCorrelations.Nusselt(re, 5, 0.02);
            Assert.True(nu >= prev * 0.999, $"Nu({re}) = {nu} меньше предыдущего {prev}");
            prev = nu;
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SiederTate_Clamped_At_366()
    {
        Assert.Equal(3.66, KCorrelations.NusseltSiederTate(50, 1, 0.001), 9);
    }
}

public class PressureDropTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void RelativeDp_Scales_As_v175()
    {
        double dp1 = PressureDrop.RelativeDp(0.05, 1.0, 1.0, 850, 850, 0.003, 0.003);
        double dp2 = PressureDrop.RelativeDp(0.05, 2.0, 1.0, 850, 850, 0.003, 0.003);
        Assert.Equal(Math.Pow(2, 1.75), dp2 / dp1, 4);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void RelativeDp_Monotonic_In_Velocity()
    {
        double prev = 0;
        for (double v = 0.1; v <= 5.0; v += 0.1)
        {
            double dp = PressureDrop.RelativeDp(0.05, v, 1.0, 850, 850, 0.003, 0.003);
            Assert.True(dp >= prev, $"dP({v}) = {dp} меньше предыдущего {prev}");
            prev = dp;
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SpeedRelOut_Matches_P4()
    {
        // v ниже минимума: (v_min − v)/v_min
        Assert.Equal(0.4, PressureDrop.SpeedRelOut(0.3, 0.5, 2.0), 9);
        // v выше максимума: (v − v_max)/v_max
        Assert.Equal(0.15, PressureDrop.SpeedRelOut(2.3, 0.5, 2.0), 9);
        // в диапазоне — 0
        Assert.Equal(0, PressureDrop.SpeedRelOut(1.0, 0.5, 2.0), 9);
    }
}

public class FinePhysicsTests
{
    private static EnvironmentSpec Crude() => new()
    {
        Id = "NFT", Name = "Нефть-тест", Kind = "liquid", Petroleum = true, TminC = 15, TmaxC = 260,
        Points = new PropertyPoint[]
        {
            new(20, 1.95, 870, 8.0e-2, 0.130),
            new(60, 2.10, 850, 2.0e-2, 0.124),
            new(100, 2.25, 830, 7.0e-3, 0.118),
            new(150, 2.40, 805, 3.0e-3, 0.110),
        },
    };

    private static ProjectSpec Project()
    {
        var envs = new List<EnvironmentSpec>(Pinchess.Engine.Props.DefaultEnvironments.Create()) { Crude() };
        return new ProjectSpec
        {
            ProjectName = "fine-тест",
            Environments = envs,
            Flows = new[]
            {
                new FlowSpec { Id = "F1", IsHot = true, EnvironmentId = "NFT", FlowRateKgH = 100_000,
                    TStartC = 150, TTargetC = 100, AllowedApparatusIds = new[] { "A1" }, DpBudgetMpa = 0.1 },
                new FlowSpec { Id = "F2", IsHot = false, EnvironmentId = "WTR", FlowRateKgH = 50_000,
                    TStartC = 40, TTargetC = 120, AllowedApparatusIds = new[] { "A1" }, DpBudgetMpa = 0.1 },
            },
            Apparatus = new ApparatusSpec[]
            {
                new() { Id = "A1", Name = "Т-9", AreaM2 = 120, K0Wm2K = 250, TminC = 0, TmaxC = 260,
                    NominalVhMs = 1.0, NominalVcMs = 1.0, DpNomHMPa = 0.02, DpNomCMPa = 0.02 },
            },
            QglobMw = 5.0,
        };
    }

    private static FactRecord Nominal() => new("A1", 2.25, 150, 100, 40, 120);

    [Fact]
    public void Apply_Fills_ReferenceConditions()
    {
        var project = Project();
        var report = K0Calculator.Calibrate(project, new[] { Nominal() });
        var apply = K0Calculator.Apply(project, report);
        Assert.Equal(0, apply.Errors);

        var app = project.Apparatus.First(a => a.Id == "A1");
        Assert.NotNull(app.RefTHAvgC);
        Assert.NotNull(app.RefTCAvgC);
        Assert.Equal(125.0, app.RefTHAvgC!.Value, 6);   // (150+100)/2
        Assert.Equal(80.0, app.RefTCAvgC!.Value, 6);    // (40+120)/2
        Assert.NotNull(app.FlowAreaHm2);                // m/(ρ·v): 100000/3600/(850·1.0)
        Assert.InRange(app.FlowAreaHm2!.Value, 0.03, 0.04);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void FineK_HeatingReducesViscosity_RaisesRe_And_K()
    {
        var project = Project();
        var report = K0Calculator.Calibrate(project, new[] { Nominal() });
        K0Calculator.Apply(project, report);
        var app = project.Apparatus.First(a => a.Id == "A1");
        var svc = new Pinchess.Engine.Props.EnvironmentService(project);

        // новая позиция: нефть горячее (200→180 vs номинал 150→100)
        var hot = new SideContext("NFT", 100_000, 100_000, 200, 180, true);
        var cold = new SideContext("WTR", 50_000, 50_000, 40, 120, false);
        double k = FinePhysics.K(app, hot, cold, svc, 0.5, 2.0, 5.0);
        // μ нефти при 190 °C ниже, чем при 125 °C → Re выше → K' выше K0·0.5? Проверяем, что K в разумных границах и выше lo
        Assert.InRange(k, app.K0Wm2K * 0.5, app.K0Wm2K * 2.0);
        // viscosity у нефти при 190 °C экстраполяция (таблица до 150) — Walther; Re должен вырасти:
        Assert.True(k >= app.K0Wm2K * 0.99, $"K' = {k:0.0} при более горячей нефти должен быть не ниже K0·0,99");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void FineK_Uncalibrated_Is_K0_WithAnom()
    {
        var project = Project();
        var app = project.Apparatus.First(a => a.Id == "A1");
        var svc = new Pinchess.Engine.Props.EnvironmentService(project);
        var hot = new SideContext("NFT", 100_000, 100_000, 150, 120, true);
        var cold = new SideContext("WTR", 50_000, 50_000, 40, 120, false);
        double k = FinePhysics.K(app, hot, cold, svc, 0.5, 2.0, 5.0);
        Assert.Equal(app.K0Wm2K, k, 6);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Interval_Fits_Thresholds()
    {
        var app = new ApparatusSpec { Id = "X", K0Wm2K = 250 };
        var (kMin, kMax) = FinePhysics.Interval(250, app, 0.5, 2.0);
        Assert.InRange(kMin, 250 * 0.5, 250 * 0.8);
        Assert.InRange(kMax, 250 * 1.2, 250 * 2.0);
    }
}

public class FineSimulatorTests
{
    private static EnvironmentSpec Crude() => new()
    {
        Id = "NFT", Name = "Нефть-тест", Kind = "liquid", Petroleum = true, TminC = 15, TmaxC = 260,
        Points = new PropertyPoint[]
        {
            new(20, 1.95, 870, 8.0e-2, 0.130),
            new(60, 2.10, 850, 2.0e-2, 0.124),
            new(100, 2.25, 830, 7.0e-3, 0.118),
            new(150, 2.40, 805, 3.0e-3, 0.110),
        },
    };

    [Fact]
    public void Fine_Level_Converges_On_Calibrated_Scheme()
    {
        var envs = new List<EnvironmentSpec>(Pinchess.Engine.Props.DefaultEnvironments.Create()) { Crude() };
        var project = new ProjectSpec
        {
            ProjectName = "fine-sim",
            Environments = envs,
            Flows = new[]
            {
                new FlowSpec { Id = "F1", IsHot = true, EnvironmentId = "NFT", FlowRateKgH = 100_000,
                    TStartC = 150, TTargetC = 100, AllowedApparatusIds = new[] { "A1" }, DpBudgetMpa = 0.1 },
                new FlowSpec { Id = "F2", IsHot = false, EnvironmentId = "WTR", FlowRateKgH = 50_000,
                    TStartC = 40, TTargetC = 120, AllowedApparatusIds = new[] { "A1" }, DpBudgetMpa = 0.1 },
            },
            Apparatus = new ApparatusSpec[]
            {
                new() { Id = "A1", AreaM2 = 120, K0Wm2K = 250, TminC = 0, TmaxC = 260,
                    NominalVhMs = 1.0, NominalVcMs = 1.0, RefTHAvgC = 125, RefTCAvgC = 80,
                    FlowAreaHm2 = 0.033, FlowAreaCm2 = 0.033 },
            },
            QglobMw = 5.0,
        };
        var committed = new[]
        {
            new AppInstance("A1", "F1", Side.Hot, 0, 1.0, false, "P0"),
            new AppInstance("A1", "F2", Side.Cold, 0, 1.0, false, "P0"),
        };
        var sim = new Simulator(project);
        var coarse = sim.Simulate(committed, SimLevel.Coarse);
        var fine = sim.Simulate(committed, SimLevel.Fine);

        Assert.True(coarse.AllConverged);
        Assert.True(fine.AllConverged, $"Fine не сошёлся: {fine.FirstFailure}");
        // Fine отличается от Coarse (поправка K), но в пределах порогов [0.4·Q, 2.5·Q]
        Assert.InRange(fine.QTotalMw, coarse.QTotalMw * 0.4, coarse.QTotalMw * 2.5);
        // баланс по энтальпийным кривым обеих сторон (±1 %)
        var fa = fine.Apparatus[0];
        var hotCurve = new EnthalpyCurve(Crude());
        var waterEnv = Pinchess.Engine.Props.DefaultEnvironments.Create().First(e => e.Id == "WTR");
        var coldCurve = new EnthalpyCurve(waterEnv);
        double qHot = 100_000 * (hotCurve.H(fa.ThInC) - hotCurve.H(fa.ThOutC)) / 3.6e6;
        double qCold = 50_000 * (coldCurve.H(fa.TcOutC) - coldCurve.H(fa.TcInC)) / 3.6e6;
        Assert.InRange(qCold, qHot * 0.99, qHot * 1.01);
    }
}

using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Props;
using Pinchess.Engine.Validation;
using Xunit;

namespace Pinchess.Tests;

public class CorrelationsTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void Walther_RoundTrip_ExactAtReferencePoints()
    {
        var w = new WaltherViscosity(40, 40.0, 100, 6.0);
        Assert.Equal(40.0, w.KinematicCst(40), 6);
        Assert.Equal(6.0, w.KinematicCst(100), 6);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Walther_Decreases_With_Temperature()
    {
        var w = new WaltherViscosity(40, 40.0, 100, 6.0);
        double prev = w.KinematicCst(30);
        for (double t = 40; t <= 150; t += 10)
        {
            double next = w.KinematicCst(t);
            Assert.True(next < prev, $"ν({t}) = {next} должна быть меньше ν({t - 10}) = {prev}");
            prev = next;
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Walther_Extrapolation_Plausible()
    {
        // точки 40–100 °C, экстраполяция на 20 °C должна давать вязкость заметно выше ν(40)
        var w = new WaltherViscosity(40, 40.0, 100, 6.0);
        double nu20 = w.KinematicCst(20);
        Assert.True(nu20 > 40.0, $"ν(20) = {nu20} должна быть больше ν(40) = 40");
        double nu150 = w.KinematicCst(150);
        Assert.True(nu150 < 6.0, $"ν(150) = {nu150} должна быть меньше ν(100) = 6");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Sutherland_Air_Reference_Value()
    {
        // воздух: μ0 = 1.716e-5 при T0 = 273.15 К, S = 110.4 К → μ(20 °C) ≈ 1.81e-5
        var s = new SutherlandViscosity(1.716e-5, 273.15, 110.4);
        double mu20 = s.DynamicPaS(20);
        Assert.InRange(mu20, 1.79e-5, 1.83e-5);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PolynomialFit_RecoversQuadratic_Exactly()
    {
        // y = 0.02x² − 0.15x + 5 по точкам 0..100
        double f(double x) => 0.02 * x * x - 0.15 * x + 5.0;
        var fit = new PolynomialFit(new[] { (0.0, f(0)), (25.0, f(25)), (50.0, f(50)), (75.0, f(75)), (100.0, f(100)) }, 2);
        foreach (var x in new[] { 10.0, 33.5, 60.0, 90.0 })
            Assert.Equal(f(x), fit.Evaluate(x), 6);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TableCurve_Interpolates_And_Clips()
    {
        var c = new TableCurve(new List<(double, double)> { (10, 100), (30, 200), (50, 400) });
        Assert.Equal(150.0, c.Evaluate(20, out bool out1), 9);
        Assert.False(out1);
        Assert.Equal(350.0, c.Evaluate(45, out bool out2), 9);
        Assert.False(out2);
        Assert.Equal(100.0, c.Evaluate(5, out bool out3), 9);
        Assert.True(out3);
        Assert.Equal(400.0, c.Evaluate(60, out bool out4), 9);
        Assert.True(out4);
    }
}

public class PropertyServiceTests
{
    private static ProjectSpec ProjectWithDefaults()
    {
        var p = new ProjectSpec
        {
            ProjectName = "тест-сервис",
            Environments = DefaultEnvironments.Create(),
        };
        return p;
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Cp_At_TablePoint_IsExact()
    {
        var svc = new EnvironmentService(ProjectWithDefaults());
        // вода: точка 60 °C
        Assert.Equal(4.19, svc.CpKjKgK("WTR", 60), 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Interpolation_Between_Points_IsLinear()
    {
        var svc = new EnvironmentService(ProjectWithDefaults());
        // вода: 10→4.19, 40→4.18; при 25 °C — линейно
        double expected = 4.19 + (4.18 - 4.19) * (25 - 10) / (40 - 10);
        Assert.Equal(expected, svc.CpKjKgK("WTR", 25), 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OutsideRange_Clips_And_DoesNotThrow()
    {
        var svc = new EnvironmentService(ProjectWithDefaults());
        // вода: ниже 10 °C → значение крайней точки
        Assert.Equal(4.19, svc.CpKjKgK("WTR", 5), 9);
        Assert.Equal(1.31e-3, svc.ViscosityPaS("WTR", 2), 12);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PetroleumViscosity_ExtrapolatesViaWalther()
    {
        var svc = new EnvironmentService(ProjectWithDefaults());
        // мазут: 60 °C — 0.5 Па·с; экстраполяция на 40 °C должна быть выше
        double mu40 = svc.ViscosityPaS("FO", 40);
        Assert.True(mu40 > 0.5, $"μ_мазут(40) = {mu40} должна быть выше ν(60) = 0.5");
        // и ниже при 260 °C (за таблицей)
        double mu260 = svc.ViscosityPaS("FO", 260);
        Assert.True(mu260 < 5.0e-3, $"μ_мазут(260) = {mu260} должна быть ниже μ(250) = 5e-3");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GasViscosity_Clips_Outside_Table()
    {
        var svc = new EnvironmentService(ProjectWithDefaults());
        // природный газ: вне таблицы — клип к крайним значениям
        Assert.Equal(1.05e-5, svc.ViscosityPaS("PG", -25), 12);
        Assert.Equal(1.53e-5, svc.ViscosityPaS("PG", 210), 12);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AllDefaultEnvironments_AreValid()
    {
        var envs = DefaultEnvironments.Create();
        Assert.Equal(12, envs.Count);
        foreach (var e in envs)
        {
            Assert.True(e.Points.Count >= 3, $"Среда {e.Id}: нужно ≥3 точки");
            for (int i = 1; i < e.Points.Count; i++)
                Assert.True(e.Points[i].TC > e.Points[i - 1].TC, $"Среда {e.Id}: таблица не монотонна");
            foreach (var pt in e.Points)
            {
                Assert.True(pt.CpKjKgK > 0, $"Среда {e.Id}: cp ≤ 0");
                Assert.True(pt.DensityKgM3 > 0, $"Среда {e.Id}: ρ ≤ 0");
                Assert.True(pt.ViscosityPaS > 0, $"Среда {e.Id}: μ ≤ 0");
                Assert.True(pt.ThermalConductivityWmK > 0, $"Среда {e.Id}: λ ≤ 0");
            }
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void DefaultEnvironments_PassValidator_AsPartOfProject()
    {
        var p = ProjectWithDefaults();
        var report = ProjectValidator.Validate(p);
        // потоки/сеть пустые — ошибок по средам быть не должно
        Assert.Equal(0, report.Errors);
    }
}








=== FILE: pinchess/tests/Pinchess.Tests/PtaObjectiveTests.cs ===
using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Furnace;
using Pinchess.Engine.Objective;
using Pinchess.Engine.Search;
using Pinchess.Engine.Sim;
using Xunit;

namespace Pinchess.Tests;

/// <summary>S3 (ревью-3 2.1): PTA-каскад (ΔT_eff = Tmin − SleepTolC) и целевая функция.</summary>
public class PtaObjectiveTests
{
    private static EnvironmentSpec EnvCp1(string id, string name, double tmin, double tmax) => new()
    {
        Id = id, Name = name, Kind = "liquid", TminC = tmin, TmaxC = tmax, Petroleum = false,
        Points = new PropertyPoint[] { new(tmin, 1.0, 1000, 1e-3, 0.6), new(tmax, 1.0, 1000, 1e-3, 0.6) },
    };

    /// <summary>Контрпример ревьюера (v20): hot 100→50, cold 20→40, τ=10, мощности по 1 МВт/К.
    /// Интеграл ∫min из v20 давал 0 — каскад обязан дать ровно 20 МВт.</summary>
    private static ProjectSpec Counterexample() => new()
    {
        ProjectName = "контрпример-PTA",
        Environments = new EnvironmentSpec[] { EnvCp1("HE", "горячая среда", 40, 110), EnvCp1("CE", "холодная среда", 10, 50) },
        Flows = new FlowSpec[]
        {
            new() { Id = "H1", IsHot = true, EnvironmentId = "HE", FlowRateKgH = 3_600_000,
                    TStartC = 100, TTargetC = 50, AllowedApparatusIds = Array.Empty<string>(), DpBudgetMpa = 1.0 },
            new() { Id = "C1", IsHot = false, EnvironmentId = "CE", FlowRateKgH = 3_600_000,
                    TStartC = 20, TTargetC = 40, AllowedApparatusIds = Array.Empty<string>(), DpBudgetMpa = 1.0 },
        },
        Apparatus = Array.Empty<ApparatusSpec>(),
        QglobMw = 20.0,
    };

    [Fact]
    public void Pta_ReviewerCounterexample_Gives20()
    {
        var p = Counterexample();
        var (qRec, hotTotal, coldTotal, qHmin) = PtaCascade.QRecoverableDetailed(p, 10.0);
        Assert.Equal(50.0, hotTotal, 3);
        Assert.Equal(20.0, coldTotal, 3);
        Assert.Equal(0.0, qHmin, 3);
        Assert.Equal(20.0, qRec, 3);
    }

    // монотонность по сдвигу: чем больше ΔT, тем не больше каскадная грань
    [Fact]
    public void Pta_MoreShift_NotHigher()
    {
        var p = At5FixturesV3.FactSceneV3();
        var q6 = PtaCascade.QRecoverableMw(p, 6.0);
        var q30 = PtaCascade.QRecoverableMw(p, 30.0);
        Assert.True(q30 <= q6 + 1e-6, $"τ=30 ({q30:0.000}) > τ=6 ({q6:0.000}) — каскад не монотонен");
    }

    // глобальная грань (PTA, без множителей) покрывает beam-оптимум факт-сцены
    [Fact]
    public void UbGlobal_PtaCoversFactSceneBeam()
    {
        var p = At5FixturesV3.FactSceneV3();
        double ub = UpperBound.UbGlobalMw(p);
        Assert.True(ub >= 27.739 - 1e-6, $"глобальная грань (PTA) {ub:0.000} < beam-оптимума 27,739");
    }

    // светлая сцена: грань ≥ beam-оптимуму 8,185 (критерий Э2)
    [Fact]
    public void UbGlobal_PtaCoversLightSceneBest()
    {
        var p = At5Fixtures.Scene();
        double ub = UpperBound.UbGlobalMw(p);
        Assert.True(ub >= 8.185 - 1e-6, $"глобальная грань (PTA) лайта {ub:0.000} < Q_best=8,185");
    }

    // целевая функция: fuel-объектив монотонен по Q; QMax — бит-в-бит Q
    [Fact]
    public void Objective_FuelMonotoneInQ()
    {
        var p = At5FixturesV3.FactSceneV3();
        var fact = At5FixturesV3.FactBaseStateV3(p);
        var sim = new Simulator(p);
        var r1 = sim.Simulate(fact, SimLevel.Coarse);
        var obj = new FurnaceFuelObjective();
        Assert.Equal(r1.QTotalMw, new QMaxObjective().Score(p, r1), 9);
        Assert.True(obj.Score(p, r1) > -FurnaceModel.Compute(p, 0.0).FuelMw,
            "больше Q_сети → меньше печного топлива (монотонность нарушена)");
        // UB-отображение: UB_score ≥ Score при UB_Q ≥ Q
        double scoreUb = obj.UbFromQ(r1.QTotalMw + 1.0, p);
        Assert.True(scoreUb >= obj.Score(p, r1), "UB-критерий ниже значения критерия при UB_Q > Q");
    }
}
=== FILE: pinchess/tests/Pinchess.Tests/RulesTests.cs ===








using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
