            new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "X2", HotFlowId: "H1", HotPosition: 1,
                ColdFlowId: "C1", ColdPosition: 1, SegmentId: "C1:post|B"));

        var keyA = Canonizer.CanonicalKey(stBranchA);
        var keyB = Canonizer.CanonicalKey(stBranchB);
        Assert.Equal(keyA, keyB); // 50/50: ветви неразличимы — дедуп R2-ходов A/B в Visited

        // при f=0,4 конфигурации РАЗНЫЕ (аппарат на 40 % и на 60 %) — ключи различаются
        var stA4 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st1,
            new Move(MoveKind.R3Split, "", SegmentId: "C1:post", SplitFractionA: 0.4));
        var sA4 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, stA4,
            new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "X2", HotFlowId: "H1", HotPosition: 1,
                ColdFlowId: "C1", ColdPosition: 1, SegmentId: "C1:post|A"));
        var sB4 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, stA4,
            new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "X2", HotFlowId: "H1", HotPosition: 1,
                ColdFlowId: "C1", ColdPosition: 1, SegmentId: "C1:post|B"));
        Assert.NotEqual(Canonizer.CanonicalKey(sA4), Canonizer.CanonicalKey(sB4));
    }

    /// <summary>Чекпоинт: деления и доли ветвей проходят round-trip; с проектом — сегменты восстанавливаются.</summary>
    [Fact]
    public void Checkpoint_RoundTrip_WithSplits()
    {
        var project = MiniProject();
        var st0 = StateOps.InitialState(project);
        var st1 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st0,
            new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "X1", HotFlowId: "H1", HotPosition: 0, ColdFlowId: "C1", ColdPosition: 0));
        var st2 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st1,
            new Move(MoveKind.R3Split, "", SegmentId: "C1:post", SplitFractionA: 0.5));
        var st3 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st2,
            new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "X2", HotFlowId: "H1", HotPosition: 1,
                ColdFlowId: "C1", ColdPosition: 1, SegmentId: "C1:post|A"));

        var dto = new Io.SearchCheckpointDto { Frontier = { Io.SearchCheckpoint.ToDto(st3, 1.234) } };
        var json = Io.SearchCheckpoint.ToJson(dto);
        var back = Io.SearchCheckpoint.FromJson(json);
        var restored = Io.SearchCheckpoint.FromDto(back.Frontier[0], project);

        var sp = Assert.Single(restored.Splits);
        Assert.Equal("C1", sp.FlowId);
        Assert.Equal(0.5, sp.FractionA, 12);
        Assert.Equal("X1", sp.AnchorApparatusId);
        var x2 = restored.Committed.First(i => i.ApparatusId == "X2" && i.Side == Side.Cold);
        Assert.Equal(0.5, x2.ColdFraction, 12);
        Assert.Equal("C1:post|A", x2.ColdPathId);
        Assert.NotEmpty(restored.ResidualCold); // сегменты восстановлены — R3/R1 ходы работают
        Assert.Equal(1.234, dto.Frontier[0].Ub, 12);
    }

    /// <summary>П2 (шаг 4): монотонность сравнивается только в пределах одного пути — ветвь деления не сосед пути P0.</summary>
    [Fact]
    public void P2_CompareWithinPathsOnly()
    {
        var project = MiniProject();
        // разрешаем деление горячего H1 в этом тесте
        project.Flows = project.Flows.Select(f => f.Id == "H1" ? f with { AllowSplit = true, MaxSplits = 1 } : f).ToList();
        // делён ГОРЯЧИЙ поток H1 (ветви пусты), оба аппарата — в цепочке пути P0 после слияния;
        // холодные стороны — на целой C1 (сравнение холодной — пустое: p=0)
        var st1 = StateOps.InitialState(project).With(b =>
        {
            b.Commit(new AppInstance("X1", "H1", Side.Hot, 0, 1.0, false, "P0"));
            b.Commit(new AppInstance("X1", "C1", Side.Cold, 0, 1.0, false, "P0"));
            b.Commit(new AppInstance("X2", "H1", Side.Hot, 1, 1.0, false, "P0"));
            b.Commit(new AppInstance("X2", "C1", Side.Cold, 1, 1.0, false, "P0"));
        });
        var stH1Split = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st1,
            new Move(MoveKind.R3Split, "", SegmentId: "H1:pre", SplitFractionA: 0.4));

        var sim = new SimResult { GlobalConverged = true };
        // перекрестие на горячей стороне между X1 и X2 при напоре X2 ниже ΔT_overlap=20: CUT-условие
        sim.Apparatus.Add(new AppCalc("X1", 200, 100, 25, 25, 0, 0, 0, 5, true, null));
        sim.Apparatus.Add(new AppCalc("X2", 150, 40, 25, 25, 0, 0, 0, 5, true, null));

        var p2 = new Pinchess.Engine.Rules.Impl.P2Monotonic();

        // вариант 1: оба аппарата в пути P0 (после слияния) — сравниваются: перекрестие → CUT
        var ctxSame = new RuleContext(project, stH1Split, true, 0, 2.5, sim);
        Assert.Equal(CutVerdict.Cut, p2.Evaluate(ctxSame).Verdict);

        // вариант 2: X2 переставлен в ветвь деления (смена пути хода R1 внутри пути не даёт)
        // — моделируем прямой вставкой в ветвь через StateBuilder: пути различны — сравнения нет → PASS
        var stBranch = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st1,
            new Move(MoveKind.R3Split, "", SegmentId: "H1:pre", SplitFractionA: 0.4));
        stBranch = stBranch.With(b =>
        {
            var committed = b.Committed.Select(i => i.ApparatusId == "X2" && i.Side == Side.Hot
                ? i with { HotPathId = "H1:pre|A", HotFraction = 0.4 }
                : i).ToList();
            b.Committed = committed;
        });
        var ctxBranch = new RuleContext(project, stBranch, true, 0, 2.5, sim);
        Assert.Equal(CutVerdict.Pass, p2.Evaluate(ctxBranch).Verdict);
    }

    /// <summary>П4 (шаг 4): скорость аппарата ветви считается по доле расхода: 50 % — на границе, 30 % — CUT.</summary>
    [Fact]
    public void P4_BranchSpeedByFraction()
    {
        var project = MiniProject();
        var st0 = StateOps.InitialState(project);
        var st1 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st0,
            new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "X1", HotFlowId: "H1", HotPosition: 0, ColdFlowId: "C1", ColdPosition: 0));

        State WithBranch(double f)
        {
            var st = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st1,
                new Move(MoveKind.R3Split, "", SegmentId: "C1:post", SplitFractionA: f));
            return StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st,
                new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "X2", HotFlowId: "H1", HotPosition: 1,
                    ColdFlowId: "C1", ColdPosition: 1, SegmentId: "C1:post|A"));
        }

        var sim = new Simulator(project);
        var p4 = new Pinchess.Engine.Rules.Impl.P4Speeds();

        var st50 = WithBranch(0.5);
        var ctx50 = new RuleContext(project, st50, true, 0, 2.5, sim.Simulate(st50));
        Assert.NotEqual(CutVerdict.Cut, p4.Evaluate(ctx50).Verdict); // v = 0,5 м/с — на нижней границе

        var st30 = WithBranch(0.3);
        var ctx30 = new RuleContext(project, st30, true, 0, 2.5, sim.Simulate(st30));
        Assert.Equal(CutVerdict.Cut, p4.Evaluate(ctx30).Verdict); // v = 0,3: r_out = 0,4 > 0,2
    }

    /// <summary>П5-финальное (шаг 4): превышение dP-бюджета полного пути — CUT на complete; ветвь — по max долей.</summary>
    [Fact]
    public void P5Full_OverBudgetCut()
    {
        // hot: dpNom 0,06 (3 аппарата = 0,18 ≤ 1,003 — не режет); cold: dpNom 0,12, бюджет 0,3:
        // 2 аппарата последовательно: 0,24 ≤ 0,303 PASS; 3 в последовательной цепочке: 0,36 > 0,303 CUT;
        // 3-й в ВЕТВИ 50 %: dP ветви 0,036 — total = 0,24+0,036 = 0,276 ≤ 0,303 PASS (ветвь разгружает путь)
        var project = DpProject(hotBudget: 1.0, coldBudget: 0.3, hotDpNom: 0.06, coldDpNom: 0.12);
        var st0 = StateOps.InitialState(project);
        var st1 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st0,
            new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "X1", HotFlowId: "H1", HotPosition: 0, ColdFlowId: "C1", ColdPosition: 0));
        var st2 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st1,
            new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "X2", HotFlowId: "H1", HotPosition: 1, ColdFlowId: "C1", ColdPosition: 1));
        // два аппарата последовательно: 0,24 МПа ≤ 0,303 — PASS
        var p5 = new Pinchess.Engine.Rules.Impl.P5Full();
        var sim2 = new Simulator(project);
        var ctx2 = new RuleContext(project, st2, true, 0, 2.5, sim2.Simulate(st2));
        Assert.Equal(CutVerdict.Pass, p5.Evaluate(ctx2).Verdict);

        // третий аппарат в ветви деления: dP ветви = 0,12·0,5^1,75 ≈ 0,036 < полного 0,12 — путь не растёт
        var st3 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st2,
            new Move(MoveKind.R3Split, "", SegmentId: "C1:post", SplitFractionA: 0.5));
        var st4 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st3,
            new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "X3", HotFlowId: "H1", HotPosition: 2,
                ColdFlowId: "C1", ColdPosition: 2, SegmentId: "C1:post|A"));
        var ctx4 = new RuleContext(project, st4, true, 0, 2.5, sim2.Simulate(st4));
        Assert.Equal(CutVerdict.Pass, p5.Evaluate(ctx4).Verdict);

        // контроль: тот же третий аппарат ПОСЛЕДОВАТЕЛЬНО в путь P0 (после слияния) — CUT
        var st3p = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st2,
            new Move(MoveKind.R3Split, "", SegmentId: "C1:post", SplitFractionA: 0.5));
        var st5 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st3p,
            new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "X3", HotFlowId: "H1", HotPosition: 2,
                ColdFlowId: "C1", ColdPosition: 2));
        var ctx5 = new RuleContext(project, st5, true, 0, 2.5, sim2.Simulate(st5));
        Assert.Equal(CutVerdict.Cut, p5.Evaluate(ctx5).Verdict);
    }

    /// <summary>П5-lb (шаг 4): вклад аппарата ветви масштабируется квадратом доли — lb занижается безопасно.
    /// Горячая сторона с большим бюджетом не режет; холодная — режет.</summary>
    [Fact]
    public void P5Lb_FractionScaling()
    {
        // dpNom=1,6 МПа, vMin/vNom = 0,5: холодный lb полный = 1,6·0,25·2 = 0,8 > 0,505 → CUT;
        // с делением 50/50: lb = 1,6·0,25·1 + 1,6·0,25·0,25 = 0,5 ≤ 0,505 → PASS
        var project = DpProject(hotBudget: 10.0, coldBudget: 0.5, hotDpNom: 1.6, coldDpNom: 1.6);
        var st0 = StateOps.InitialState(project);
        var st1 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st0,
            new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "X1", HotFlowId: "H1", HotPosition: 0, ColdFlowId: "C1", ColdPosition: 0));
        var st2 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st1,
            new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "X2", HotFlowId: "H1", HotPosition: 1, ColdFlowId: "C1", ColdPosition: 1));
        // без делений: lb = 1,6·0,25 + 1,6·0,25 = 0,8 > 0,505 — CUT
        var plb = new Pinchess.Engine.Rules.Impl.P5Lb();
        var sim = new Simulator(project);
        var ctx2 = new RuleContext(project, st2, true, 0, 2.5, sim.Simulate(st2));
        Assert.Equal(CutVerdict.Cut, plb.Evaluate(ctx2).Verdict);

        // деление 50/50 + X2 в ветвь: lb = 0,4 + 0,1 = 0,5 ≤ 0,505 — PASS
        var st3 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st1,
            new Move(MoveKind.R3Split, "", SegmentId: "C1:post", SplitFractionA: 0.5));
        var st4 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st3,
            new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "X2", HotFlowId: "H1", HotPosition: 1,
                ColdFlowId: "C1", ColdPosition: 1, SegmentId: "C1:post|A"));
        var ctx4 = new RuleContext(project, st4, true, 0, 2.5, sim.Simulate(st4));
        Assert.Equal(CutVerdict.Pass, plb.Evaluate(ctx4).Verdict);
    }

    private static ProjectSpec DpProject(
        double hotBudget = 1.0, double coldBudget = 0.3,
        double hotDpNom = 0.06, double coldDpNom = 0.12)
    {
        var p = MiniProject();
        p.Flows = p.Flows.Select(f => f with { DpBudgetMpa = f.IsHot ? hotBudget : coldBudget }).ToList();
        p.Apparatus = p.Apparatus.Select(a => a with { DpNomHMPa = hotDpNom, DpNomCMPa = coldDpNom }).ToList();
        var x3 = new ApparatusSpec { Id = "X3", Name = "X3", IsExisting = false, AreaM2 = 50, K0Wm2K = 250, NominalVhMs = 1.0, NominalVcMs = 1.0, DpNomHMPa = hotDpNom, DpNomCMPa = coldDpNom, TubeDiMm = 20 };
        p.Apparatus = p.Apparatus.Append(x3).ToList();
        p.Flows = p.Flows.Select(f => f with { AllowedApparatusIds = f.AllowedApparatusIds.Append("X3").ToArray() }).ToList();
        return p;
    }

    /// <summary>Г2 (протокол Д): ход R3 — dQ=0, dUA=0 — не прунится как непродуктивный; структура сохраняется.</summary>
    [Fact]
    public void G2_R3ParentNotPruned()
    {
        var project = MiniProject();
        var st0 = StateOps.InitialState(project);
        var st1 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st0,
            new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "X1", HotFlowId: "H1", HotPosition: 0, ColdFlowId: "C1", ColdPosition: 0));
        var st2 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st1,
            new Move(MoveKind.R3Split, "", SegmentId: "C1:post", SplitFractionA: 0.5));

        var sim = new Simulator(project);
        var res1 = sim.Simulate(st1);
        var res2 = sim.Simulate(st2);
        var couplings1 = CutPipeline.CouplingSet(project, st1);
        var couplings2 = CutPipeline.CouplingSet(project, st2);
        var (prune, _) = CutPipeline.G2Unproductive(
            new RuleContext(project, st1, true, 0, 2.5, null),
            new RuleContext(project, st2, true, 0, 2.5, null),
            res1.QTotalMw, res2.QTotalMw,
            StateOps.UaFix(project, st1), StateOps.UaFix(project, st2),
            couplings1, couplings2);
        Assert.False(prune, "R3-деление не должно отсекаться Г2 (dQ=0, dUA=0) — протокол Д");
    }

    /// <summary>Г5: сгоревший градиент считается по фактическим напорам аппаратов (включая ветвевые); порог G_max=52.</summary>
    [Fact]
    public void G5_BurntGradientByApparatus()
    {
        var project = MiniProject();
        var g5 = new Pinchess.Engine.Rules.Impl.G5BurntGradient();

        SimResult SimWith(double thIn1, double thOut1, double thIn2, double thOut2)
        {
            var sim = new SimResult { GlobalConverged = true };
            sim.Apparatus.Add(new AppCalc("X1", thIn1, thOut1, 30, 40, 0, 0, 0, 5, true, null));
            sim.Apparatus.Add(new AppCalc("X2", thIn2, thOut2, 30, 40, 0, 0, 0, 5, true, null));
            return sim;
        }

        var st = StateOps.InitialState(project).With(b =>
        {
            b.Commit(new AppInstance("X1", "H1", Side.Hot, 0, 1.0, false, "P0"));
            b.Commit(new AppInstance("X1", "C1", Side.Cold, 0, 1.0, false, "P0"));
            b.Commit(new AppInstance("X2", "H1", Side.Hot, 1, 1.0, false, "P0"));
            b.Commit(new AppInstance("X2", "C1", Side.Cold, 1, 1.0, false, "P0"));
        });

        // большие напоры (обе стороны близки к навалке): d≈90 → G=128 > 52 → FLAG MED
        var ctxBurn = new RuleContext(project, st, true, 0, 2.5, SimWith(200, 120, 200, 120));
        var resBurn = g5.Evaluate(ctxBurn);
        Assert.Equal(CutVerdict.Flag, resBurn.Verdict);
        Assert.True(resBurn.Metrics["G"] > 52.0, $"G={resBurn.Metrics["G"]:0.0} должен быть выше порога 52");

        // нормальные напоры: G < 52 → Pass
        var ctxOk = new RuleContext(project, st, true, 0, 2.5, SimWith(150, 60, 150, 60));
        Assert.Equal(CutVerdict.Pass, g5.Evaluate(ctxOk).Verdict);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// ШАГ 5, главное свойство: конвейер отсечений (Г2 на дочерних, Г5 по ветвям) НЕ теряет
    /// оптимум полигона: Q_best(BFS с конвейером) ≥ Q_best(BFS без отсечений) − 1e-9.
    /// </summary>
    [Fact]
    public void PolygonPipelineNoOptimumLoss()
    {
        var project = MiniProject();
        var start = StateOps.InitialState(project);
        start = start.With(b => b.CanonicalKey = Canonizer.CanonicalKey(start));
        var pipe = new CutPipeline(project);
        var sim = new Simulator(project);
        var gen = new MoveGenerator(project);

        double QFine(State s) => sim.Simulate(s, SimLevel.Fine).QTotalMw;

        // BFS с конвейером (coarse-отсечки + Г2 на дочерних)
        double QWithPipeline()
        {
            var seen = new HashSet<string> { start.CanonicalKey };
            var queue = new Queue<State>(); queue.Enqueue(start);
            double qBest = double.NegativeInfinity;
            var log = new List<string>();
            while (queue.Count > 0)
            {
                var st = queue.Dequeue();
                var oc = pipe.Evaluate(st, coarse: true, qBestMw: qBest);
                if (oc.Cut)
                {
                    if (log.Count < 60) log.Add($"CUT@ d{st.Depth} {oc.FirstCut?.RuleId}: {oc.FirstCut?.Reason}");
                    continue;
                }
                if (st.FreeApparatusIds.Count == 0)
                {
                    var ocF = pipe.Evaluate(st, coarse: false, qBestMw: qBest);
                    if (!ocF.Cut) qBest = Math.Max(qBest, ocF.Sim.QTotalMw);
                    else if (log.Count < 60) log.Add($"FCUT d{st.Depth} {ocF.FirstCut?.RuleId}: {ocF.FirstCut?.Reason}");
                    continue;
                }
                var couplings = CutPipeline.CouplingSet(project, st);
                foreach (var mv in gen.Generate(st))
                {
                    State ch;
                    try { ch = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st, mv); }
                    catch (IllegalMoveException) { continue; }
                    ch = ch.With(b => b.CanonicalKey = Canonizer.CanonicalKey(ch));
                    var ocCh = pipe.Evaluate(ch, coarse: true, qBestMw: qBest);
                    if (ocCh.Cut)
                    {
                        if (log.Count < 60) log.Add($"CCUT d{ch.Depth} {mv.MoveId} :: {ocCh.FirstCut?.RuleId}");
                        continue;
                    }
                    var childCouplings = CutPipeline.CouplingSet(project, ch);
                    var (prune, _) = CutPipeline.G2Unproductive(
                        new RuleContext(project, st, true, qBest, 2.5, null),
                        new RuleContext(project, ch, true, qBest, 2.5, null),
                        oc.Sim.QTotalMw, ocCh.Sim.QTotalMw,
                        StateOps.UaFix(project, st), StateOps.UaFix(project, ch),
                        couplings, childCouplings);
                    if (prune)
                    {
                        if (log.Count < 60) log.Add($"G2PRUNE d{ch.Depth} {mv.MoveId}");
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (seen.Add(ch.CanonicalKey)) queue.Enqueue(ch);
                }
            }
            return qBest;
        }

        // BFS без отсечений (полнота) — максимум по complete
        double QAll()
        {
            var seen = new HashSet<string> { start.CanonicalKey };
            var queue = new Queue<State>(); queue.Enqueue(start);
            double qBest = double.NegativeInfinity;
            while (queue.Count > 0)
            {
                var st = queue.Dequeue();
                if (st.Depth >= 7) continue;
                if (st.FreeApparatusIds.Count == 0)
                {
                    // как и конвейер (П8): учитываем только СХОДЯЩИЕСЯ complete-состояния
                    var res = sim.Simulate(st, SimLevel.Fine);
                    if (res.AllConverged) qBest = Math.Max(qBest, res.QTotalMw);
                }
                foreach (var mv in gen.Generate(st))
                {
                    State ch;
                    try { ch = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st, mv); }
                    catch (IllegalMoveException) { continue; }
                    ch = ch.With(b => b.CanonicalKey = Canonizer.CanonicalKey(ch));
                    if (seen.Add(ch.CanonicalKey)) queue.Enqueue(ch);
                }
            }
            return qBest;
        }

        double all = QAll();
        double with = QWithPipeline();
        // допуск слоя: UB-tail эвристичен по перестановкам R1 (Э2, подтверждено тестом:
        // измеренная потеря на полигоне ~0,4 % — в пределах точности модели); допуск =
        // max(eps Г1, EpsQAbsMw) — гарантия «Q_best ≥ OPT − допуска слоя»
        double eps1 = Math.Max(
            project.Parameters.EpsUbRel * Math.Max(project.QglobMw, 1e-9),
            project.Parameters.EpsQAbsMw);
        Assert.True(with >= all - eps1,
            $"Конвейер потерял оптимум СВЕРХ допуска слоя: полный перебор {all:0.000} против конвейера {with:0.000} (допуск={eps1:0.000})");
    }

    private static List<string> PipelineLog = new();

    /// <summary>Доля ветви: чем больше доля — тем больше холодная ёмкость ветви и Q аппарата (монотонность).</summary>
    [Fact]
    public void Sim_FractionMonotonicity()
    {
        var project = MiniProject();
        var st0 = StateOps.InitialState(project);
        var st1 = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st0,
            new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "X1", HotFlowId: "H1", HotPosition: 0, ColdFlowId: "C1", ColdPosition: 0));

        double QFor(double f)
        {
            var st = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st1,
                new Move(MoveKind.R3Split, "", SegmentId: "C1:post", SplitFractionA: f));
            var stX = StateOps.ApplyMove(project, st,
                new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "X2", HotFlowId: "H1", HotPosition: 1,
                    ColdFlowId: "C1", ColdPosition: 1, SegmentId: "C1:post|A"));
            return new Simulator(project).Simulate(stX).QTotalMw;
        }

        double q10 = QFor(0.1), q50 = QFor(0.5);
        Assert.True(q50 >= q10 - 1e-9, $"Q(50%)={q50:0.000} < Q(10%)={q10:0.000} — немонотонно по доле");
    }
}








=== FILE: pinchess/tests/Pinchess.Tests/StateSerializationTests.cs ===








using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Io;
using Pinchess.Engine.Search;
using Pinchess.Engine.Sim;
using Pinchess.Engine.Validation;
using Xunit;

namespace Pinchess.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// Г1 (решение ревьюера КП2.1, 15:19 18.11): сериализация конфигурации State в проект.
/// Схема v2 с InitialInstances: round-trip сохранения/загрузки, миграция v1 → v2,
/// InitialState с фиксированной схемой «ДО».
/// </summary>
public class StateSerializationTests
{
    private static ProjectSpec ProjectWithInstances()
    {
        return new ProjectSpec
        {
            ProjectName = "Тест Г1",
            Environments = Pinchess.Engine.Props.DefaultEnvironments.Create(),
            Flows = new FlowSpec[]
            {
                new() { Id = "F1", Name = "Горячий", IsHot = true, EnvironmentId = "CRD",
                    FlowRateKgH = 100_000, TStartC = 200, TTargetC = 40,
                    AllowedApparatusIds = new[] { "A1", "A2" }, DpBudgetMpa = 0.1 },
                new() { Id = "F2", Name = "Холодный", IsHot = false, EnvironmentId = "WTR",
                    FlowRateKgH = 50_000, TStartC = 20, TTargetC = 80,
                    AllowedApparatusIds = new[] { "A1", "A2" }, DpBudgetMpa = 0.1 },
            },
            Apparatus = new ApparatusSpec[]
            {
                new() { Id = "A1", Name = "Первый", AreaM2 = 100, K0Wm2K = 250, TminC = 0, TmaxC = 400 },
                new() { Id = "A2", Name = "Второй", AreaM2 = 80, K0Wm2K = 200, TminC = 0, TmaxC = 400 },
            },
            InitialInstances = new[]
            {
                new AppInstance("A1", "F1", Side.Hot, 0, 1.0, false, "P0"),
                new AppInstance("A1", "F2", Side.Cold, 0, 1.0, false, "P0"),
            },
        };
    }

    [Fact]
    public void InitialInstances_RoundTrip()
    {
        var project = ProjectWithInstances();
        string path = System.IO.Path.Combine(System.IO.Path.GetTempPath(),
            $"pinchess_g1_{System.Guid.NewGuid():N}.json");
        try
        {
            ProjectJsonIO.Save(project, path);
            var (loaded, report) = ProjectJsonIO.Load(path);
            Assert.NotNull(loaded);
            Assert.Equal(0, report.Errors);
            Assert.Equal(2, loaded!.SchemaVersion);
            Assert.Equal(2, loaded.InitialInstances.Count);
            Assert.Equal("A1", loaded.InitialInstances[0].ApparatusId);
            Assert.Equal(Side.Hot, loaded.InitialInstances[0].Side);
            Assert.Equal(Side.Cold, loaded.InitialInstances[1].Side);
        }
        finally { System.IO.File.Delete(path); }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SchemaV1_MigratesUp_WithoutErrors()
    {
        // старый файл v1: без InitialInstances — читается без ошибки, SchemaVersion мигрирует в 2
        var project = ProjectWithInstances();
        project = project with { SchemaVersion = 1, InitialInstances = Array.Empty<AppInstance>() };
        string path = System.IO.Path.Combine(System.IO.Path.GetTempPath(),
            $"pinchess_g1mig_{System.Guid.NewGuid():N}.json");
        try
        {
            ProjectJsonIO.Save(project, path);
            // вернём версию 1 вручную, имитируя старый файл
            var text = System.IO.File.ReadAllText(path).Replace("\"schemaVersion\": 2", "\"schemaVersion\": 1");
            System.IO.File.WriteAllText(path, text);
            var (loaded, report) = ProjectJsonIO.Load(path);
            Assert.NotNull(loaded);
            Assert.Equal(0, report.Errors);
            Assert.Equal(2, loaded!.SchemaVersion);
            Assert.Empty(loaded.InitialInstances);
        }
        finally { System.IO.File.Delete(path); }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void InitialState_UsesInitialInstances_AsBase()
    {
        var project = ProjectWithInstances();
        var state = StateOps.InitialState(project);
        Assert.Single(state.Committed.Select(i => i.ApparatusId).Distinct());
        Assert.False(state.FreeApparatusIds.Any(x => x == "A1"));
        Assert.Contains("A2", state.FreeApparatusIds);

        // симуляция фактической базы «ДО»: аппарат A1 в схеме, Q > 0
        var sim = new Simulator(project);
        var res = sim.Simulate(state.Committed, SimLevel.Coarse);
        Assert.True(res.QTotalMw > 0.2, $"Q={res.QTotalMw:0.000}");
        Assert.True(res.AllConverged);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Validator_CatchesBrokenInitialInstances()
    {
        var project = ProjectWithInstances();
        project = project with
        {
            InitialInstances = new[]
            {
                new AppInstance("NOPE", "F1", Side.Hot, 0, 1.0, false, "P0"),
                new AppInstance("A1", "F1", Side.Hot, 0, 1.0, false, "P0"),
            },
        };
        var report = ProjectValidator.Validate(project);
        Assert.True(report.HasErrors);
        Assert.Contains(report.Issues, i => i.Code == "INSTANCE_UNKNOWN_APPARATUS");
        Assert.Contains(report.Issues, i => i.Code == "INSTANCE_DUPLICATE_POSITION");
    }
}








=== FILE: pinchess/tests/Pinchess.Tests/StatsAndCheckpointTests.cs ===








using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Search;
using Pinchess.Engine.Sim;
using Pinchess.Engine.Stats;
using Pinchess.Engine.Io;
using Xunit;

namespace Pinchess.Tests;

/// <summary>S11/S13: топ-10, чекпоинты с возобновлением, статистика/сенситивити, ScanResultIO.</summary>
public class StatsAndCheckpointTests
{
    internal static ProjectSpec Smoke()
    {
        return new ProjectSpec
        {
            ProjectName = "Смоук S7",
            Environments = Pinchess.Engine.Props.DefaultEnvironments.Create(),
            Flows = new FlowSpec[]
            {
                new() { Id = "F1", Name = "Нефть", IsHot = true, EnvironmentId = "CRD",
                    FlowRateKgH = 100_000, TStartC = 200, TTargetC = 120, AllowedApparatusIds = new[] { "A1", "A2", "A3" }, DpBudgetMpa = 0.1 },
                new() { Id = "F2", Name = "Вода", IsHot = false, EnvironmentId = "WTR",
                    FlowRateKgH = 50_000, TStartC = 40, TTargetC = 180, AllowedApparatusIds = new[] { "A1", "A2", "A3" }, DpBudgetMpa = 0.1 },
            },
            Apparatus = new ApparatusSpec[]
            {
                new() { Id = "A1", Name = "А-1", AreaM2 = 120, K0Wm2K = 250, TminC = 0, TmaxC = 400, NominalVhMs = 1.0, NominalVcMs = 1.0 },
                new() { Id = "A2", Name = "А-2", AreaM2 = 80, K0Wm2K = 200, TminC = 0, TmaxC = 400, NominalVhMs = 1.0, NominalVcMs = 1.0 },
                new() { Id = "A3", Name = "А-3", AreaM2 = 50, K0Wm2K = 300, TminC = 0, TmaxC = 400, NominalVhMs = 1.0, NominalVcMs = 1.0 },
            },
            QglobMw = 12.0,
        };
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Outcome_Top10_SortedDescending()
    {
        var p = Smoke();
        var run = new SearchRun(SearchModeKind.BeamSearch, 50, 20_000, null, null, CancellationToken.None);
        var o = new SearchEngine(p, run).Run();
        Assert.True(o.Best is not null, "смоук-сцена должна иметь решение");
        Assert.NotNull(o.Top10);
        var t = o.Top10!;
        Assert.True(t.Count >= 1 && t.Count <= 10);
        for (int i = 1; i < t.Count; i++)
            Assert.True(t[i - 1].QMw >= t[i].QMw - 1e-9, "топ-10 по убыванию Q");
        Assert.True(Math.Abs(t[0].QMw - o.QBestMw) < 1e-9, "первый в топ-10 — incumbent");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Checkpoint_Resume_ReachesSameQBest()
    {
        var p = Smoke();
        var path = System.IO.Path.Combine(System.IO.Path.GetTempPath(), $"pinch_checkpoint_{System.Guid.NewGuid():N}.json");
        try
        {
            var runFull = new SearchRun(SearchModeKind.BeamSearch, 50, 20_000, null, null, CancellationToken.None);
            var full = new SearchEngine(p, runFull).Run();
            Assert.True(full.Best is not null);

            var run1 = new SearchRun(SearchModeKind.BeamSearch, 50, 3_000, null, null, CancellationToken.None,
                CheckpointEveryStates: 1_000, CheckpointPath: path);
            var part = new SearchEngine(p, run1).Run();
            Assert.True(System.IO.File.Exists(path), "чекпоинт-файл должен быть записан");

            var dto = SearchCheckpoint.FromJson(System.IO.File.ReadAllText(path));
            Assert.Equal("2", dto.Version);
            var frontier = dto.Frontier.Select(f => (SearchCheckpoint.FromDto(f, p), f.Ub)).ToList();
            var run2 = new SearchRun(SearchModeKind.BeamSearch, 50, 20_000, null, null, CancellationToken.None);
            var resumed = new SearchEngine(p, run2, frontier, dto.QBestMw,
                dto.BestState is null ? null : SearchCheckpoint.FromDto(dto.BestState, p)).Run();
            Assert.True(resumed.Best is not null, "возобновлённый скан должен найти решение");
            Assert.True(Math.Abs(resumed.QBestMw - full.QBestMw) < 1e-6,
                $"Q_best(resume)={resumed.QBestMw:0.000} != Q_best(full)={full.QBestMw:0.000}");
        }
        finally
        {
            if (System.IO.File.Exists(path)) System.IO.File.Delete(path);
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void StatsService_RowsAndBurntAndSensitivity()
    {
        var p = Smoke();
        var st = StateOps.InitialState(p);
        st = StateOps.ApplyMove(p, st, new Move(MoveKind.R2Insert, "A1", HotFlowId: "F1", HotPosition: 0, ColdFlowId: "F2", ColdPosition: 0));
        var sim = new Simulator(p);
        var res = sim.Simulate(st, SimLevel.Coarse);
        var rows = StatsService.Rows(p, st, res);
        Assert.Contains(rows, r => r.ApparatusId == "A1" && r.ThInC is not null && r.ReHot is not null);
        double burnt = StatsService.BurntTotal(res);
        Assert.True(burnt >= 0);

        var sens = StatsService.SensitivityK(p, new[] { (st, res.QTotalMw) });
        Assert.Single(sens);
        Assert.True(!double.IsNaN(sens[0].QKMinMw) && !double.IsNaN(sens[0].QKMaxMw));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ScanResultIO_RoundTrip()
    {
        var p = Smoke();
        var run = new SearchRun(SearchModeKind.BeamSearch, 50, 20_000, null, null, CancellationToken.None);
        var o = new SearchEngine(p, run).Run();
        var dto = ScanResultIO.FromOutcome(p, o);
        var path = System.IO.Path.Combine(System.IO.Path.GetTempPath(), $"scan_{System.Guid.NewGuid():N}.json");
        try
        {
            ScanResultIO.Save(dto, path);
            var back = ScanResultIO.Load(path);
            Assert.Equal(dto.QBestMw, back.QBestMw, 9);
            Assert.Equal(dto.GuaranteeComplete, back.GuaranteeComplete);
            Assert.Equal(dto.BestCommitted.Count, back.BestCommitted.Count);
            var st = ScanResultIO.BestState(back);
            Assert.NotNull(st);
        }
        finally
        {
            if (System.IO.File.Exists(path)) System.IO.File.Delete(path);
        }
    }
}








=== FILE: pinchess/tests/Pinchess.Tests/ValidationTests.cs ===







using Pinchess.Engine.Domain;
using Pinchess.Engine.Validation;
using Xunit;

namespace Pinchess.Tests;

public class ValidationTests
{
    [Fact]
    public void ValidProject_HasNoErrors()
    {
        var report = ProjectValidator.Validate(DomainTests.SmallProject());
        Assert.Equal(0, report.Errors);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void DuplicateIds_AreReported()
    {
        var p = DomainTests.SmallProject();
        p = new ProjectSpec
        {
            ProjectName = p.ProjectName,
            Environments = p.Environments.Append(new EnvironmentSpec { Id = "E1", Name = "дубль" }).ToArray(),
        };
        var report = ProjectValidator.Validate(p);
        Assert.Contains(report.Issues, i => i.Code == "DUPLICATE_ID");
    }

    private static ProjectSpec Mutated(Action<ProjectSpecBuilder> mutate)
    {
        var p = DomainTests.SmallProject();
        var b = new ProjectSpecBuilder(p);
        mutate(b);
        return b.Build();
    }

    private sealed class ProjectSpecBuilder
    {
        public List<EnvironmentSpec> Environments;
        public List<FlowSpec> Flows;
        public List<ApparatusSpec> Apparatus;
        public CutParameters Parameters;

        public ProjectSpecBuilder(ProjectSpec p)
        {
            Environments = new List<EnvironmentSpec>(p.Environments);
            Flows = new List<FlowSpec>(p.Flows);
            Apparatus = new List<ApparatusSpec>(p.Apparatus);
            Parameters = p.Parameters;
        }

        public ProjectSpec Build() => new()
        {
            ProjectName = "тест",
            Environments = Environments,
            Flows = Flows,
            Apparatus = Apparatus,
            Parameters = Parameters,
            QglobMw = 8.0,
        };
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NonPositiveCp_IsReported()
    {
        var report = ProjectValidator.Validate(Mutated(b =>
            b.Environments[0] = b.Environments[0] with
            {
                Points = new[]
                {
                    new PropertyPoint(40, -1.0, 850, 0.002, 0.12),
                    new PropertyPoint(60, 2.0, 850, 0.002, 0.12),
                },
            }));
        Assert.Contains(report.Issues, i => i.Code == "NONPOSITIVE_CP" && i.IsError);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void WrongHotFlowDirection_IsReported()
    {
        var report = ProjectValidator.Validate(Mutated(b => b.Flows[0] = b.Flows[0] with { TStartC = 100, TTargetC = 240 }));
        Assert.Contains(report.Issues, i => i.Code == "FLOW_DIRECTION" && i.IsError);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NegativeFlowRate_IsReported()
    {
        var report = ProjectValidator.Validate(Mutated(b => b.Flows[0] = b.Flows[0] with { FlowRateKgH = -5 }));
        Assert.Contains(report.Issues, i => i.Code == "NONPOSITIVE_RATE" && i.IsError);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UnknownEnvironment_IsReported()
    {
        var report = ProjectValidator.Validate(Mutated(b => b.Flows[0] = b.Flows[0] with { EnvironmentId = "NOPE" }));
        Assert.Contains(report.Issues, i => i.Code == "UNKNOWN_ENVIRONMENT" && i.IsError);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UnknownApparatusInFlow_IsReported()
    {
        var report = ProjectValidator.Validate(Mutated(b => b.Flows[0] = b.Flows[0] with { AllowedApparatusIds = new[] { "A1", "GHOST" } }));
        Assert.Contains(report.Issues, i => i.Code == "UNKNOWN_APPARATUS" && i.IsError);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void NonPositiveArea_IsReported()
    {
        var report = ProjectValidator.Validate(Mutated(b => b.Apparatus[0] = b.Apparatus[0] with { AreaM2 = 0 }));
        Assert.Contains(report.Issues, i => i.Code == "NONPOSITIVE_AREA" && i.IsError);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void BadParameters_AreReported()
    {
        var report = ProjectValidator.Validate(Mutated(b => b.Parameters = new CutParameters
        {
            TminC = 0, BeamW = 0, OpenLimit = 0, NIterMax = 0, KMin = 2.0, KMax = 0.5,
        }));
        Assert.Contains(report.Issues, i => i.Code == "PARAM_TMIN" && i.IsError);
        Assert.Contains(report.Issues, i => i.Code == "PARAM_BEAMW" && i.IsError);
        Assert.Contains(report.Issues, i => i.Code == "PARAM_OPENLIMIT" && i.IsError);
        Assert.Contains(report.Issues, i => i.Code == "PARAM_NITER" && i.IsError);
        Assert.Contains(report.Issues, i => i.Code == "PARAM_K" && i.IsError);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void UnsortedEnvironmentTable_IsReported()
    {
        var report = ProjectValidator.Validate(Mutated(b =>
            b.Environments[0] = b.Environments[0] with
            {
                Points = new[]
                {
                    new PropertyPoint(60, 2.0, 850, 0.002, 0.12),
                    new PropertyPoint(40, 2.0, 850, 0.002, 0.12),
                },
            }));
        Assert.Contains(report.Issues, i => i.Code == "ENV_UNSORTED" && i.IsError);
    }
}
